Input material for P12 audit; reduces audit's discovery surface so the audit can focus on resolution and methodology synthesis.
  - Commit 2 (`docs(readme)`, f9c41f1) expands the repository-root README per the four operator-confirmed dispositions. Methodology framing repositioned as living hypothesis revised by session data points and audit findings (replacing the stale "pending operator authorship per D39" framing). `charter/schema.md` and `charter/deferred-decisions.md` added to the charter reading order at positions 5 and 6 (schema after decisions per the binding-specification pairing; deferred-decisions after schema as the activation-trigger surface). Apache 2.0 repositioned under a new "The strategic commitment" section: "The platform is the demonstration; the methodology is the proprietary insight" — open-sourcing as load-bearing for the bet. Two new sections: "What the platform does at Phase 1 close" enumerating the six producer contexts (agent, ingestion, run_history, audit, retrieval_evaluation, optimization) plus the two applications (cli, api); "Operating the platform" covering install dependencies, start the stack via `make up`, apply migrations via `make migrate`, seed dev tenants via `make seed-tenants`, exercise the CLI surface via `docker compose exec padhanam-api python -m apps.cli.main` plus the three Makefile shortcuts (eval-run / ingest-run / ingest-worker), exercise the HTTP surface at `https://localhost/openapi.json` with reference to the S42 smoke walkthrough.
  - Commit 3 (`chore(p11/hygiene)`, db285f8) refreshes the compose.yaml padhanam-api digest pin from sha256:fa829a07277c... to sha256:aa21431690ab... via re-running `make build-api`. The post-S41 target's S42-fix (commit ac5a6bf, replacing `docker compose build` with direct `docker build` to avoid docker compose build's rejection of digest-tagged image directives) holds; the refresh is the verification step rather than a new fix. Same commit removes the stray `apps/api/{routers}/` literal-braces empty directory (dated 2026-04-30, surfaced at S42 Finding 1 investigation as a fat-finger artefact unrelated to the actual routers/ tree). The directory removal is invisible to git but the on-disk state is now clean.
  - Commit 4 (`docs(charter)`, 44f3897) lands the graph-extract pipeline reliability structural finding at `charter/deferred-decisions.md` per Finding 1 disposition. The EXTRACTING state in `contexts/ingestion/domain/state.py` has no reclaim-after-timeout transition; a worker dying mid-extraction (LLM-call hang, container kill, transaction-rollback edge case) leaves the source stuck in EXTRACTING indefinitely. The extraction LLM call at `contexts/ingestion/adapters/outbound/extraction/litellm_extractor.py` has no timeout enforcement at the worker boundary either. Three structural pieces missing: reclaim policy (EXTRACTING-for-longer-than-timeout → PENDING_EXTRACT with retry counter, terminal EXTRACTION_FAILED at reclaim_limit_exceeded), worker-side asyncio timeout enforcement around the extractor call, optimistic-concurrency-control on the reclaim transition for split-brain safety against concurrent workers. Operational mitigation at Phase 1: manual re-trigger via CLI (SELECT then forced UPDATE of stuck sources; procurement-grade-invisible because no audit event emission — part of the reliability finding the structural reclaim policy resolves). Activation trigger: any Phase 1 close or Phase 2 work that depends on graph retrieval producing entities reliably; production deployment with multiple worker processes amplifies the failure mode.
  - Commit 5 (`fix(p11/hygiene)`, 4dc4c8c) loosens the S37 audit reader validator at `contexts/audit/domain/audit_event_record.py` to accept empty correlation_id as legitimate "no inbound HTTP context" signal. The original S37/D102 commitment required non-empty correlation_id across all six text fields; cross-context audit semantics drift surfaced at S42 smoke time when audit rows emitted by the retrieval_evaluation runner (S40/D110) and the optimization engine (S41/D111) carried correlation_id="" because engine-internal events have no inbound HTTP request context. The S37 reader's list route returned 500 against any tenant_audit chain containing such rows. The loosening preserves all other invariants (five required-non-empty text fields, the type contract on correlation_id, the 64-lowercase-hex hash validation, the tenant_id shape commitment); two new unit tests verify the accept path and the still-raises-on-None path. The recommended-(a) disposition from the S42 captures entry held; the alternative-(b) backfill migration was rejected because the empty-string state is structurally meaningful and a backfill would inject false data to satisfy an overly-strict validator.
  - Commit 6 (this commit) lands the session log entry plus the close marker on `charter/current-package.md`.

- Decisions: None at this session. Hygiene work surfaces structural findings (graph-extract reliability lands at `charter/deferred-decisions.md` per the brief's disposition rule, not as a new D-entry) and methodology candidates (consolidated at `charter/p12-audit-inputs.md` for P12 to resolve). No new charter principles; no new D-entries.

- Tests: 1213 unit tests pass at session close (+1 from S42's 1212; one parametrize case removed for correlation_id, two new explicit tests added — net +1). 148 tenant_isolation contract scenarios unchanged; 18 pagination/error contract scenarios unchanged; 33 integration api tests unchanged. 27 import-linter contracts kept.

- Enforcement layer: No new contracts. The hygiene session ships no new architectural surface; the validator loosening is a one-line change preserving the type contract; the structural finding is documented rather than implemented.

- Findings outcomes captured per the brief's reflection prompts:

  - **Finding 1 (graph-extract investigation outcome).** Structural, not bounded. The investigation surfaced the EXTRACTING state's lack of reclaim-after-timeout transition as the root cause. A worker dying mid-extraction leaves the source stuck indefinitely; no other worker picks the row up. The extraction LLM call has no worker-boundary timeout either. The bounded-fix possibilities the brief named (worker timeout adjustment alone, retry semantics layered on top of existing transitions, queue-state edge case) were inadequate because they don't address the missing reclaim transition; a worker-side timeout adjustment alone leaves the row in EXTRACTION_FAILED on every transient failure (which inflates failure rates against real reliability), and retry semantics layered on EXTRACTION_FAILED would require a separate reset-failed-to-pending transition that doesn't exist. The structural reclaim policy is the structurally honest fix; bounded fixes would be incidental scope expansion masquerading as the right shape. The disposition's bounded-or-document rule held cleanly.

  - **Finding 2 (CLI/API wiring parity outcome).** Defensible asymmetry; no backfill needed. The brief's premise of `tests/integration/apps/api/test_wiring.py` doesn't exist (path mismatch; api tests live at `tests/integration/api/`), and neither side has a single dedicated `test_wiring.py` file. The actual surfaces:
    - CLI side: explicit cross-context adapter tests at `tests/integration/apps/cli/test_cross_context_adapters.py` (eight test functions covering the methodology/role/source/agent-retrieval adapters) and `tests/integration/apps/cli/test_cross_context_tool_adapters.py` (tool-definitions and tool-invoker adapters) plus dedicated reader-adapter tests at `test_run_history_reader_adapter.py` and `test_audit_event_reader_adapter.py`. The pattern: construct adapters with fake repositories and verify the consumer-port-plus-wiring-adapter boundary translates correctly.
    - API side: route-level integration tests at `tests/integration/api/test_*_routes.py` (eight test files post-S42) that exercise the wiring through TestClient plus FastAPI dependency_overrides. The pattern: substitute the adapter at the dependency-injection seam and verify the route behaves correctly end-to-end.
    The asymmetry is defensible because the two surfaces test wiring at different altitudes appropriate to each app's entry point. CLI-side tests verify adapter-construction correctness at the consumer-port-plus-wiring-adapter boundary (where the CLI's per-command construction model means adapter shape matters); API-side tests verify the same wiring through the full HTTP stack (where FastAPI dependency injection plus the request-scoped session resolution model means stack-shape matters more than construction-shape). No backfill needed for procurement-grade defensibility.

- methodology (line 1): **Hygiene-session-as-shape methodology observation.** This is the first explicit hygiene-shaped session in P11. Distinct from substrate sessions (ship architectural surface — S39, S40, S41), bridge sessions (verification-and-hygiene between substrate sessions — S39b, S40b), and transport sessions (HTTP layer atop existing substrate — S42). Hygiene sessions consolidate end-of-package debt: documentation expansion, methodology-candidate consolidation, dev-tooling verification, structural-finding documentation, stray-artefact cleanup. The shape's distinguishing characteristics that surfaced at execution: smaller per-commit scope than substrate sessions (six commits, each tightly scoped); mixed commit prefixes per work nature (`docs(charter)`, `docs(readme)`, `chore(p11/hygiene)`, `fix(p11/hygiene)`); no new D-entries (the hygiene session does not produce binding architectural commitments); structural findings forwarded to deferred-decisions rather than fixed in-session per the bounded-fix-or-document disposition rule. Open question for P12 audit: is the hygiene-shaped session pattern materially different from a bridge session, or is it a bridge session at a different temporal position (P11 close versus mid-substrate-package)? My read at session close: it's distinct because the temporal position changes what work qualifies as "hygiene". Mid-package bridges address substrate-session debt (corpus state, gold-set authoring contamination); end-of-package hygiene addresses cross-package debt (methodology-candidate consolidation, README expansion, structural findings that accumulated across multiple sessions). Forward-relevance for Phase 2: hygiene sessions at phase boundaries should be planned by default rather than reactive; the methodology candidate at `charter/p12-audit-inputs.md` entry 14 captures the question.

- methodology (line 2): **Investigation outcomes summary — bounded-fix-or-document rule held cleanly.** Two findings surfaced at this session; both followed the brief's disposition rule without operator engagement. Finding 1 (graph-extract) surfaced as structural after inspection of `contexts/ingestion/domain/state.py` and `contexts/ingestion/application/extract_source.py`; the bounded-fix-possibilities the brief named were genuinely inadequate, not surface-level "this looks bigger than expected" misjudgment. Finding 2 (CLI/API wiring parity) surfaced as the brief's premise being partially wrong (no `test_wiring.py` files at either side) but the underlying concern (parity coverage across the eighteen wiring class pairs) being met by different test surfaces at different altitudes. Both outcomes are structurally honest, not "this is too hard so document" deflection. The disposition rule's value at this session: it gave CC a clear escalation criterion (engage operator only if structural finding requires substantive refactor exceeding the bounded-fix budget OR if backfill scope exceeds ~50 lines) without which the temptation to bound the graph-extract fix at the worker-timeout-adjustment surface might have been substantial.

- methodology (line 3): **Methodology-candidates consolidation observations.** The `charter/p12-audit-inputs.md` file is the first structured input set for P12 audit. The fourteen-entry structured-entry format (observation + evidence + decision shape implied) surfaced a clean separation between entries that are clearly methodology-document candidates (entries 1, 2, 3, 4, 14: pre-write reconciliation, principle-versus-framing drift, scope-check-at-substrate-application-boundary, bridge-session pattern, hygiene-session-as-shape), entries that are clearly charter-amendment candidates (entries 5, 6: vendor-flexibility operationalisation verdict, container-image-lag pattern closed), entries that are clearly deferred-decisions candidates (entries 8, 9, 10, 13: cost-threshold tuning, parallel_rrf, gold-set audit emission, graph-extract reliability), and entries that are explicit-non-action candidates (entries 7, 11, 12: MRR threshold lesson is one-time, S39b corpus version-control gap is captured-not-repeating, CLI/API wiring asymmetry is defensible). The clean separation suggests the structured format does support P12 audit's resolution work without biasing toward certain disposition types. Forward-relevance: P12 audit may add audit-surfaced entries (e.g., observations the audit conversation itself surfaces about session-shape distribution or methodology-pattern recurrence rates); the file's append-only-between-audits convention with the P12-resolved archive section at audit close keeps the file useful across phases without unbounded growth.

- **P11 session-level work closed.** Pre-P12 hygiene is the last session of P11; the next strategic-mode block is the P12 phase audit. P12 acts on the fourteen entries at `charter/p12-audit-inputs.md` plus any audit-surfaced additions per the four disposition types. The audit's output artefact updates `charter/p12-audit-inputs.md` with per-entry disposition annotations plus any new entries; charter-amendment dispositions land at the appropriate charter file (decisions.md, principles.md, schema.md, deferred-decisions.md) in the same audit-close commit chain. The Phase 1 close audit then assesses Phase 1's procurement-grade-readiness across the full surface (P1 through P12); this hygiene session's outputs flow into that assessment.

Pydantic discriminated union on evidence_citations keyed by `category` literal mirrors the domain-layer union shape.

- methodology (line 1): **Pre-write reconciliation surfacing brief-vs-codebase deviation reaches fifth instance in P11.** Finding 5 (DTO placement) deviation was the recurrence at S42. The pattern now has five P11 instances (S39 D109 vs codebase substrate; S40 D66 vs as-built strategies; S40b graph-extract; S41 D111 framing scope; S42 Finding 5 DTO placement). The previous methodology line at S41 already named this beyond promotion threshold; S42 reinforces.

- methodology (line 2): **Smoke-time verification of dev-workflow tooling at the same session that ships it.** New candidate at log/captures.md 2026-05-15 [S42] entry. The post-S41 `make build-api` target was authored at commit 0e8041f without being exercised against the production-shape flow (the commit's own validation used `docker build` directly, the established pattern). The first production-shaped use at S42 smoke surfaced the docker-compose-build-rejects-digest-tag bug. Structurally invisible to unit tests (Makefile is not test-covered); only smoke-time exercise catches the class. The candidate is "when a session ships dev-workflow tooling, the same session's smoke must exercise the new tooling end-to-end" — applies recursively when the tooling is itself smoke-time infrastructure. Recurrence test continues at the next dev-workflow tooling addition (likely pre-P12 hygiene or P12 territory).

- methodology (line 3): **Audit-row data shape inconsistency surfaces at smoke time as cross-context audit semantics drift.** New captures entry at log/captures.md 2026-05-15 [S42]. The S40 retrieval_evaluation runner and the S41 optimization engine emit audit events with `correlation_id=""` (no inbound HTTP request context for engine-internal work); the S36/S37 audit reader's `AuditEventRecord.__post_init__` validator rejects empty correlation_id. The list route's 500 at smoke-time Stage 8 is the surfaced symptom. The structural lesson: cross-context audit semantics need explicit thinking about "is every audit event tied to an inbound HTTP request?" — answer is no (engine internals, background work), so the validator's commitment to non-empty correlation_id is too strict. Recommended one-line validator loosening at pre-P12 hygiene. The captures entry carries the recommendation plus the alternative (backfill migration to set sentinel correlation_id values) and the structurally honest argument for the looser validator.

- methodology (line 4): **OpenAPI-as-procurement-grade-deliverable verdict.** S42 is the first session committing OpenAPI specification as a deliverable, not just a side-effect of FastAPI route definitions. Stage 9 of the smoke verified all 17 S42 operationIds present in /openapi.json; the spec mirrors the FastAPI route declarations 1:1 with no maintenance burden because the route definitions ARE the spec source. A Phase 2 frontend developer reads /openapi.json and integrates without needing source access. Procurement-grade defensibility: spec-vs-implementation divergence is structurally impossible because FastAPI generates the spec from the route definitions that drive runtime behaviour. Forward-relevance for Phase 2: when OpenAPI consumer-tooling matures (TypeScript codegen, contract testing), this substrate is ready without rework.

- methodology (line 5): **HTTP transport surface session shape distinct from substrate sessions.** Methodology candidate observation: S42 is the first session in the P11 sequence (after S39 / S39b / S40 / S40b / S41) that ships HTTP transport over an existing substrate rather than ship the substrate itself. Substantively different shape — smaller architectural surface (no new ports, no new domain types, no new adapters; just inbound adapter layer atop existing application use cases); more execution-shaped (routes, DTOs, query parsers, error handlers, wiring extensions, tests). The pre-write reconciliation discipline still mattered (Finding 1 scope investigation reduced session size; Finding 5 deviation caught the structural-consistency call); the substrate-application-boundary scope-check pattern from S41 did not fire because there was no substrate to land. The role-functions exercised distribute differently — heavy engineer-and-PM, lighter analyst-and-architect (no new architectural decisions beyond D112 itself), tech-writer normal. P12 audit input: should HTTP-transport sessions be a named session shape with distinct discipline expectations versus substrate sessions?

- **Procurement-grade UX-consumer surface verdict.** A Phase 2 frontend developer reads `/openapi.json` (17/17 S42 operationIds present at smoke Stage 9; total 30 operations across the full API) and integrates against the platform without needing to read source. The four-context P11 substrate scaffold's consumer surface is complete: run_history (S34 per D98), audit (S37 per D103), retrieval_evaluation (S42 commit 3), optimization (S42 commit 4). The two-step gold-set discovery decomposition preserves human-in-the-loop content-fit discipline at the HTTP layer per S40b precedent. The recommendation lifecycle through HTTP lands equivalently to the S41 CLI exercise. Tenant isolation through HTTP holds with no information leakage. Phase 2 frontend work can build directly against this substrate without rework at the HTTP layer.

- **P11 close.** S42 closes P11's build-session sequence: D108 framed the four-context-substrate-plus-HTTP-transport bet at P11 framing; D109 / D110 / D111 / D112 land the four substrates plus their consumer HTTP surface across S39 / S39b / S40 / S40b / S41 / S42. Pre-P12 hygiene is the next strategic-mode block: README expansion; methodology candidate consolidation (the captures entries from this run plus the strong P11 candidates: pre-write reconciliation, principle-versus-framing drift, scope-check-at-substrate-application-boundary, smoke-time-verification-of-dev-tooling, OpenAPI-as-procurement-deliverable, HTTP-transport-session-shape); audit-row correlation_id loosening; the stray `apps/api/{routers}/` empty directory hygiene; the captures-promote-to-methodology window before the P12 phase audit itself.

Demonstrates the discipline's value extends beyond drift-catching into scope-shape reasoning. Strong P12 audit promotion candidate; the candidate was already at promotion threshold per S40b's three-instance count.

The brief framed `contexts/optimization/domain/rules/` against no specific as-built reality; the framing simply contradicted the hexagonal layering principle the codebase commits to at D16. No pre-write reconciliation against codebase reality could catch it because there was no codebase reference to reconcile against; only writing the code and watching the import pattern surface the principle violation catches the drift. Mitigation surface different from the as-built-versus-as-framed drift previously surfaced — closer to "check the framing against the principles file before writing the prompt." Surfaced again at commit 8 when the TYPE_CHECKING import broke the layers-optimization contract; same root class (principle violation in framing that only surfaces at write time). Two instances in S41 alone reinforces the candidate. P12 audit input.

- methodology (line 3): **Scope-check-at-substrate-application-boundary as candidate methodology default.** Captured at `log/captures.md` mid-session. The original S41 brief framed 12 commits; the (δ) push-back at pre-write reconciliation expanded scope ~15-20%. Mid-execution operator pause caught the scope-versus-framing divergence at the natural substrate-application boundary (between commit 3 closing the domain layer and commit 4 opening the application work). Whether substrate sessions should plan a scope-check at the domain-application boundary by default rather than discovering scope creep at execution time is the P12 audit question. The candidate is the planned-scope-check moment, not the pause itself.

- methodology (line 4): **Container-image-lag pattern at smoke time.** Third instance in P11: S39 also had `make migrate` against an outdated container; S40 had the audit-adapter-connection-not-yet-in-container issue; S41 has the migration-file-not-in-container issue. The fixing-inline-at-smoke methodology established at S40 (operator's flagged-CLI-audit-connection-risk disposition: fix inline, capture as methodology finding) worked again here — `docker compose cp` the migration plus the new context tree plus modified CLI/wiring modules into the running container, then `make migrate`. Container-rebuild would be the production-shaped path; the dev-rig smoke pattern uses cp-into-running. Three instances in P11; promotion candidate — should the dev workflow include a documented "fast-path code sync into running container for smoke" step? Or should the operator-driven smoke loop assume container rebuild as baseline and accept the slower turnaround?

- methodology (line 5): **Vendor-flexibility principle operationalisation verdict.** S41 is the first session to operationalise the new vendor-flexibility principle beyond vendor ports via pluggable domain-layer abstractions (MetricCalculator + RecommendationRule). Substantive verdict: the abstractions feel structurally honest, not over-engineered. MetricCalculator's two-method shape was an explicit Finding 1 expansion away from the original single-method draft because the runner consumes both surfaces; the wider Protocol matches the actual consumption pattern. RecommendationRule's single `evaluate` method matches its actual consumption pattern (engine calls it once per registered rule). Phase 2 graded-relevance implementations (nDCG, MAP) and richer rule-trigger languages have clean swap surfaces without runner change. Forward-relevance for Phase 2: evaluation-unit shape for graded relevance is the next natural pluggable-abstraction candidate when scoring-sheet substrate ships; rule-trigger language is the candidate when user-defined-rules surfaces consumer demand.

- methodology (line 6): **Four-context P11 substrate scaffold close at S41.** Gold-set substrate (S39 + S39b) → runner (S40 + S40b clean-gold-set bridge) → optimization (S41). The bridge-session-shape pattern's second instance closed at S40b; S41 did not require its own bridge — the substrate-application boundary pause at commit 3 close functioned as an in-session structural-honesty checkpoint instead. Whether the bridge-session pattern can be absorbed into in-session checkpoints at the substrate-application boundary (per methodology line 3) is the P12 audit question. The four-context scaffold is procurement-grade complete: every producer context owns its consumer-defined reader port, the optimization layer wraps the four readers via EvidenceContext, audit-event-level tamper-evidence absorbs the optimization lifecycle per D110's three-regime distinction extended at D111 commitment 8.

- S41-close verdict on procurement-grade defensibility: **PASS.** A procurement reader looking at recommendation `9f3e311d-...` from the smoke can trace the full chain without ambiguity. Prose names the action and magnitude. Rule provenance is implicit in the category (`retrieval_strategy` → `RetrievalStrategyRule` per D111 commitment 5) and the rule's trigger semantics are explicit in code. Citation evidence carries `evaluation_run_id=c168c2ba` (S40b's contamination-broken run; verifiable directly) and `gold_set_id=3b001430` (S40b's clean gold-set). Caveat annotation flags graph_only's all-zero aggregates with `caveat_code=infrastructure_substrate_check_required` — substrate transparency, not silent masking. Audit chain (7 events × full evidence_citations embedded) ensures the lifecycle has not been tampered with. Substrate transparency for Phase 1 zero categories (model_choice, prompt_revision) is queryable at both the OptimizationRun aggregate and the audit event level. The bet's success criterion 4 (the trace capture layer surfaces optimization recommendations, not just data) is demonstrated end-to-end against tenant_a's real evidence; D108's four-category commitment is procurement-grade-honest about Phase 1 scope without amendment.

## S40b — Clean gold-set authoring with content-fit selection
roles: engineer, technical writer
mode: build (verification-and-hygiene bridge between S40 runner close and S41 optimization framing)

- Produced: Five commits closed S40b.
  - Commit 1 (`docs(charter)`, ad9d729) lands the S40b in-flight paragraph at `charter/current-package.md` and the `.gitignore` rule for `docs/archive/snapshots/` closing the AC 17 cleanliness gap S40 flagged.

Vocabulary diverges from the LVTGuide system prompt on case-study-specific terms (Pacelane, recovery-first, HRV, Spearman correlation, retention, subscription) while keeping unavoidable LVT-conceptual overlap (bet, initiative, epic, story, success criteria). The corpus is version-controlled so chunk references are reproducible across DB rebuilds, closing the S39b corpus-not-in-repo gap prospectively for S40b; S39b's corpus stays uncommitted as a separate P12 audit input.
  - Commit 3 (`docs(p11/s40b)`, 221ef1c) lands the live-stack smoke at `docs/smoke/p11_s40b_clean_gold_set.md` with five-stage executed-evidence walkthrough. Stage 1 (corpus refresh): Neo4j cleanup per the operator's refined criterion (12 entities trimmed, 8 deleted), Postgres delete + new source ingest, graph-extract bypass via forced state='indexed' captured as methodology finding plus orphan-entity cleanup after a late-completing worker. Stage 2 (S39b rename): direct SQL UPDATE renaming the rank-selected gold-set; hash byte-identical post-rename per the canonical-payload-excludes-name proof. Stage 3 (clean gold-set authoring): create `3b001430-...`, three per-query content-fit selection blocks each capturing the chunks selected, the chunks rejected, and the criterion that drove each line including the transparent borderline-section judgment for Query 2; finalize with revision hash `8fec2553d9...`. Stage 4 (verification re-run): runner produces `c168c2ba-...` with substantive recall@k drops vs S39b. Stage 5 (S41-evidence verdict): contamination broke at the recall@k surface.
  - Commit 4 (this commit) lands the session log entry plus the S40b close marker at `charter/current-package.md`.

- Decisions: No new D-entries. Two `charter/deferred-decisions.md` entries from S40 (`parallel_rrf` retrieval-strategy implementation; gold-set aggregate-level audit emission) remain active without S40b touch. The methodology principle S40b demonstrates — selection function at authoring must differ from the function being evaluated — is forwarded to P12 audit as a candidate, not committed as a D-entry inside S40b per the brief's explicit out-of-scope item.

- Tests: 1105 unit tests pass at session close (matches S40 baseline; no test surface touched at S40b). 26 import-linter contracts kept. Tenant_isolation contract harness scenario count unchanged at 126 (no new ports or adapter shapes; the harness extension at commit 8f179fd covers what S40b exercises).

- Enforcement layer: No `import-linter` contract changes. No AST enforcement changes. The runtime fix from S40 (`PostgresAuditAdapter.from_settings` at commit 21acd36) is the only code surface the verification re-run depends on; S40b exercises the runner CLI end-to-end without further code modifications.

- Operator-decided dispositions at pre-write reconciliation:
  - **Finding 1 (corpus refresh scope):** Option (a) — replace `lvt_methodology_overview.md` with the Pacelane case study. Operator strengthening accepted: Query 2 reframing is load-bearing alongside corpus replacement because the word *framing* in the original Query 2 is system-prompt vocabulary. Reframed to "how did Pacelane apply LVT to its recovery-first wearable launch" using case-study-specific terms. Queries 1 and 3 unchanged.
  - **Finding 2 (authoring agent provenance):** Option (a) — CC autonomously authors with content-fit selection. The structural-honesty cut is selection-function-must-differ-from-evaluated-function, not selection-is-human; CC reading chunk content and judging answer-fit meets the cut.
  - **Finding 3 (S39b gold-set rename):** Option (a) — direct SQL UPDATE on `gold_sets.name`. Renamed to "P11 retrieval baseline (rank-selected, S39b)". Stored revision hash `9ee5aed07c7c...` byte-identical post-rename per the canonical-payload-excludes-name proof from S39b's smoke Stage 3.
  - **Finding 4 (corpus durability):** Option (a) — version-controlled at `tests/fixtures/corpus/p11_s40b/`. Closes the S39b corpus-not-in-repo gap prospectively.
  - **Finding 5 (Ollama state; autonomous):** Compose stack healthy at S40 smoke; `nomic-embed-text:v1.5` present. No drift since S39b.
  - **Finding 6 (replace-vs-additive; refined to refined-cleanup-criterion):** Ingest CLI is additive-only; cleanup of LVT chunks + entities required direct DB operations. Operator's refined Neo4j criterion landed: trim `source_chunk_ids` to remove deleted chunk references first, conditional-delete only when array is empty. Preserved "bet", "Padhanam", "PM agent", "agent" entities (co-derived from surviving sources); deleted 8 LVT-source-only entities.
  - **Finding 7 (gold-set audit-emission gap; autonomous informational):** Confirmed; no S40b touch.

- methodology (line 1): **Pre-write reconciliation surfacing brief-vs-required-structure gaps reaches third instance in P11.** S39 Finding 2 caught D105's scoring-sheet-precedent failure (sibling-in-placement-not-in-pattern). S40 Finding 2 caught D66's framing-vs-as-built drift (catalogue says three strategies, adapter executes two). S40b pre-write reconciliation caught the Query 2 reframing being load-bearing alongside corpus replacement — the operator's brief named the corpus refresh but did not name the query vocabulary as system-prompt-overlapping. Three instances inside one package. The methodology candidate from S40 line 1 strengthens at S40b: pre-write reconciliation routinely surfaces drift between strategic-mode framing and as-built or as-required structure; the discipline's value is now demonstrable at three instances in P11 alone. Strong P12 audit promotion candidate.

- methodology (line 2): **Metric-threshold expectations should derive from structural understanding of what each metric measures, not from gut intuition.** The brief stated MRR above 0.9 would trigger corpus revision. At Stage 4 the recall@k drops were substantive (28% relative at k=1, 20% at k=3) but MRR stayed at 1.0. Operator-owned at smoke time as a wrong implicit assumption: content-fit selection typically agrees with vector retrieval at rank-1 when vector retrieval is competent at top-1 ranking, so MRR=1.0 is a structural property of this metric on a competent-retrieval setup, not a contamination signal. The structural cut sharper than the brief framed: at S39b rank-1 is in expected BY CONSTRUCTION (rank-based selection); at S40b rank-1 is in expected BY JUDGMENT (content-fit happened to agree with vector's top-1 because vector's top-1 is genuinely relevant). The selection-must-differ-from-evaluated-function principle holds in both cases; MRR just isn't the surface where the difference surfaces. Recall@k is the contamination-sensitive surface in this evaluation setup. P12 audit candidate: metric-threshold expectations derive from structural understanding of what each metric measures, not from gut intuition about what "clean" looks like. The principle generalises to any evaluation context with multiple metric surfaces.

- methodology (line 3): **Graph-extract pipeline reliability is the gating constraint for any S40-style smoke that needs both retrieval legs.** The local Ollama qwen2.5:7b extract pipeline is slow (1-3 min per chunk) and unreliable on this dev rig — multiple worker invocations left the Pacelane source in `extracting` state without progress, and a late-completing worker wrote orphan entities for a deleted source that required separate cleanup. Per the S40 CLI runner's honest-empty graph_only leg, S40b's contamination-break test doesn't depend on Neo4j entity creation; bypassed by forcing `state='indexed'`. P12 audit input: invest in extract reliability (timeout handling, automatic retry semantics, observable worker progress) at a future hygiene session, or accept that graph_only metrics are honest-zero on this rig and document the expectation. Both paths reasonable; S40b operates under the second.

- methodology (line 4): **Bridge-session-shape pattern's second instance close.** S39 → S39b was verification-and-hygiene (LVTGuide absent, corpus empty, CLI not runtime-verified). S40 → S40b was methodologically-clean-artefact authoring (S39b gold-set was rank-selected; S40b corrected with content-fit). Both bridge sessions resolved specific structural-honesty gaps the substrate session surfaced but could not fix without scope creep. The pattern (S39b methodology line 5) now has two confirmed instances against different sub-substrate concerns. The candidate at S40 close was "one more instance at P11 close or P12 would promote"; S40b is the second instance. Promotion threshold met for P12 audit consideration: bridge sessions as a distinct session shape between substrate and audit with two named sub-substrate variants. Substantive paragraph for the audit: whether substrate sessions should plan bridges in by default rather than discovering them at substrate close is the question P12 should resolve; the brief flagged this explicitly.

- methodology (line 5): **S41 framing brief should privilege recall@k differentials over MRR explicitly in evidence-citation specs.** Operator commitment from the verdict-disposition disposition: when S41 framing happens, the evidence-citation specification for the `retrieval_strategy` recommendation category names recall@k and precision@k as the load-bearing metric surfaces, not MRR. A recommendation like "switch from vector_only to graph_only because vector_only's recall@3 of 0.80 underperforms graph_only's recall@3 of 0.92 on this gold-set" cites the right surface; the same recommendation citing MRR=1.0 differentials would not (MRR is structurally non-discriminating in this evaluation setup). Worth flagging in S41's brief explicitly so the evidence-shape commitment is correct from the start. This is a near-term carryover from S40b for S41 framing, not a P12 audit candidate.

- S40b-close verdict on S41-evidence credibility: **The S40b run `c168c2ba-328f-4163-b374-1f69d914b623` can be cited as procurement-grade evidence for retrieval-strategy recommendations.** The contamination at S39b (selection-function-equals-evaluation-function via rank-based authoring) broke at the recall@k surface where it should surface: recall@1 dropped from 0.555 to 0.400 (28% relative), recall@3 from 1.000 to 0.800 (20%), recall@5 from 1.000 to 0.867 (13%). Vector retrieval is now measured against an independent ground-truth selection function. MRR stayed at 1.0 for structural reasons (operator-owned threshold-setting error captured at methodology line 2); recall@k carries the contamination-break verdict instead. S41 proceeds with the load-bearing-metric commitment captured at methodology line 5.

- Decisions: D110 landed at commit 1, revised per pre-write reconciliation. Seven commitments. Two new `charter/deferred-decisions.md` entries: `parallel_rrf` retrieval-strategy implementation (activates at P12 audit if procurement-grade three-strategy comparison surfaces, or at a Phase 2 retrieval-adapter-extension session) and gold-set aggregate-level audit emission (activates no later than the Phase 1 close audit). The two carryovers preserve the framing-vs-as-built divergence loops without rewriting D66 or back-filling S39's gold-set substrate inside S40 scope.

- Tests: 1105 unit tests pass at session close (+58 from S39b's 1047). 19 new runner-application unit tests; 39 new domain-level tests across `evaluation_run`/`evaluation_result`/`evaluation_aggregate`/`metrics` (committed at a76919b). 26 import-linter contracts kept. Tenant_isolation contract harness extends 114 → 126 (+12 new scenarios at S40).

- Enforcement layer: No new `import-linter` contracts; the retrieval_evaluation layers contract from S39 continues to govern the new application/ports/adapter modules. No new AST enforcement. The hexagonal layer order (adapters → application → ports → domain) holds across all new code; the new `RetrievalRunnerPort` and `EvaluationRunRepository`/`EvaluationRunReader` ports satisfy the `ports/` layer-purity expectation (Protocol classes, no SQLAlchemy, no asyncpg).

- Operator-decided dispositions at pre-write reconciliation:
  - **Finding 1 (run granularity):** Option (a) — one run × all executing strategies. Comparison surface co-located inside one EvaluationRun; optimization layer at S41 cites one run_id as evidence covering every executing strategy. Brief recommendation held.
  - **Finding 2 (strategy catalogue):** Option (a) — ship two strategies at S40; `parallel_rrf` deferred to Phase 2 via new `charter/deferred-decisions.md` entry. D110 commitment 6 reframes from "all three" to "every D66-registered strategy with an executing branch in `AgentRetrievalClientAdapter` at S40 close". D110 alternatives (h) and (i) explicitly reject implement-now and rewrite-D66 paths. Substantive operator-prose disposition; reshaped D110 inline. Methodology candidate at line 1 below.
  - **Finding 3 (invocation path):** Option (a) — runner invokes via `AgentRetrievalClientAdapter` callable as the single dispatch site. Canonical-identifier projection rule (`vector_only` → `{"primary": "vector"}`; `graph_only` → `{"primary": "graph"}`) lives at `application/strategy_keys.py`, not at the adapter; runner consumes, adapter unchanged.
  - **Finding 4 (metrics library; autonomous):** No existing implementation; custom land at `domain/metrics.py` per the brief recommendation.
  - **Finding 5 (latency capture; autonomous):** Wall-clock from invocation-start to result-return via `time.monotonic()`; not reading OTel spans (decouples runner from observability instrumentation, which the runner explicitly does not invoke per D110 commitment 5).
  - **Finding 6 (audit-event emission):** **Not autonomous; surfaced for operator decision.** Verification showed S39's retrieval_evaluation context emits zero audit events on gold-set writes; gold-set tamper-evidence sits on chain-self-contained revision hashes per D26. D110 commitment 7 introduces audit emission for the first time in the context, creating intra-context asymmetry. Operator confirmed option (A) with the three-regime distinction reasoning: tenant-authored content sits under hash-chain self-containment (D26's revision-shaped pattern); actor-driven events and platform-computed records sit under the audit-event chain (also D26). Gold-set audit emission gap activates as a separate carryover, not in-S40 scope. Methodology candidate at line 2 below.
  - **Finding 7 (wiring patterns; autonomous):** 12 existing wiring classes at `apps/cli/_cross_context.py`; the two new adapters extend cleanly as #13 and #14.

- methodology (line 1): **D66 strategy registry framing-vs-as-built divergence surfaced at pre-write reconciliation.** Finding 2 surfaced that D66 catalogues `vector_only` / `graph_only` / `parallel_rrf` as a three-strategy starter set, but `AgentRetrievalClientAdapter` at `apps/cli/_cross_context.py:322-462` dispatches on `{"primary": "vector"}` and `{"primary": "graph"}` only — the third "strategy" key (`{"primary": "vector", "secondary": "graph"}` in the adapter's docstring) is documented as Phase 1 best-effort with no fusion-merge code. The operator's prose disposition kept D66 unchanged and added a new `charter/deferred-decisions.md` entry naming the gap. This is the **third** instance of framing-vs-as-built drift surfacing at P11 pre-write reconciliation (after D105's scoring-sheet-precedent failure at S39 Finding 2 and the field-set-agnostic primitive misidentification at S39 mid-build reconciliation). Three instances is a pattern. Strong methodology candidate at the P12 audit for "pre-write reconciliation as the load-bearing discipline for catching framing-vs-as-built drift between sessions". Adjacent observation: the strategy-key surface at the adapter is stringly-typed (`Mapping[str, Any]`) — naming the keys in a structured way (enum or typed identifier) is a separate concern worth a P12 audit note but out of scope for S40.

- methodology (line 2): **First audit-event emission inside `contexts/retrieval_evaluation/` establishes the three-regime distinction.** D110 commitment 7 introduces audit emission to the context where gold-sets (S39) did not emit; the reasoning paragraph names three D26 regimes explicitly: tenant-authored content under hash-chain self-containment (methodology revisions, gold-set revisions), actor-driven events under the audit-event chain (tenancy registry mutations, agent-runtime mutations), and platform-computed records under the audit-event chain mirroring the actor regime (the runner's `evaluation_runs`/`results`/`aggregates`). The intra-context asymmetry (gold-set writes don't emit; runner writes do) is structurally honest under this regime distinction — gold-sets are tenant-authored, runner records are platform-computed. The gold-set audit-emission gap activates as a separate carryover. **P12 audit input:** the three-regime distinction generalises beyond retrieval_evaluation and may want explicit naming at `charter/principles.md` if a fourth bounded context surfaces the same regime question; current evidence sits at two contexts (audit + retrieval_evaluation), one more would promote.

- methodology (line 3): **Bridge-session-shape pattern confirms at second instance.** S39 surfaced an S39b verification-and-hygiene bridge carryover (LVTGuide absent, corpus empty, CLI not runtime-verified); S40 surfaces an S40b methodologically-clean-gold-set authoring bridge carryover (S39b gold-set is rank-based-not-content-fit so its metrics cannot discriminate between strategies). Both bridge sessions resolve specific structural-honesty gaps the substrate session surfaced but cannot fix without scope creep. The pattern (S39b methodology line 5) now has two instances against different sub-substrate concerns: verification-and-hygiene at S39b, methodologically-clean-artefact authoring at S40b. One more instance at P11 close or P12 would promote to a formal `charter/methodology.md` entry — bridge sessions as a distinct session shape between substrate and audit.

- methodology (line 4): **Substrate-plus-runner-co-located bounded-context discipline confirmed; runner-at-substrate-context is the right cut.** D110 commitment 1 placed the runner at `contexts/retrieval_evaluation/` alongside the gold-set substrate from S39, rejecting placement at `contexts/evaluation/` (scoring-sheet pattern) or a new context. The build experience confirms the choice: the runner reuses `GoldSetReader` directly via the existing port (no cross-context substrate coupling), the runner's tables sit on the same per-tenant DB as the gold-sets they exercise (FK enforcement makes cross-tenant association structurally impossible per the contract harness's run_persist_blocked test), and the consumer-port-plus-wiring-adapter pattern reaches the thirteenth and fourteenth instances without architectural impedance. The brief flagged "does runner-at-substrate-context produce friction, or is it the right cut?" — the cut is the right cut. **Methodology candidate:** P12 audit may want to name this as "co-locate substrate and orchestrator-against-substrate inside one bounded context" when the orchestrator reads its inputs from the substrate; the alternative (substrate at one context, orchestrator at a sibling) mirrors D109's sibling-in-placement-not-in-pattern failure mode. Adjacent observation: the no-adapter-unit-tests pattern from S39 (verification lives at the contract harness + smoke) continued at S40; this is now the retrieval_evaluation-specific test-pattern asymmetry against the API composition root, flagged at S39 methodology line 4 and still standing.

- methodology (line 5): **Verification-debt-from-smoke-carryovers pattern's second instance at retrieval_evaluation.** S39b methodology line 1 named the pattern: substrate-session CLI ACs cannot claim closure on import-shape verification alone; runtime verification at the smoke surface catches what import-shape checks structurally cannot. S40 smoke fired the second instance: `_build_runner_dependencies` constructed the audit adapter with `ControlPlaneSettings.url_async` (a non-existent attribute). Unit tests cannot catch this because the fakes (`RecordingAuditPort`) substitute the adapter wholesale. Fixed inline at commit 21acd36 by switching to `PostgresAuditAdapter.from_settings(...)` matching the production composition path. Adjacent observation: CLI wiring helpers that construct production-class adapters inline (vs delegating to existing factory functions) are a specific sub-pattern worth naming at the P12 audit; the API composition root at `apps/api/_agent_runtime_wiring.py` uses `from_settings` everywhere; the CLI's inline construction is the structural-honesty gap.

- methodology (line 6): **Operator feedback on AskUserQuestion option-list constraint for findings that reshape D-entries.** At Findings 1-3 surfacing I posed via AskUserQuestion with structured options; the operator rejected the tool call and responded in free-form prose that added load-bearing specificity my option labels could not have anticipated (exact deferred-decisions text, specific alternative-rejection text, canonical-identifier projection rule with placement, explicit non-rewrite-D66 commitment). The feedback saved to `~/.claude/projects/-Users-sabu-padhanam/memory/feedback_prose_over_option_lists_for_findings.md` per the auto-memory convention. Pattern: when a finding may change D-entry text or add a new charter entry, narrate in assistant message text and pause for free-form operator response; reserve AskUserQuestion for narrow self-contained decisions where labels fully cover the space. Signal-to-detect: if option descriptions become paragraphs rather than short labels, the question is too rich for the tool. The methodology line is captured here for the session record; the memory entry persists across conversations.

- S40-close verdict on the S39b gold-set's S41 usability: **An S40b clean-gold-set bridge session is required before S41 ships.** The runner substrate works structurally end-to-end (smoke Stage 1-5 evidence). The metrics it produced against the S39b real-corpus gold-set are not procurement-grade S41 evidence: the gold-set's `expected_chunk_ids` were rank-1 selected from vector retrieval at S39b authoring time, and the runner at S40 invokes the same vector retrieval at evaluation time, so MRR=1.0 and recall@1=high are the artefact of two identical functions on the same corpus, not retrieval-quality measurement. For S41's optimization engine to cite `recall_at_5 = 0.42` as evidence that a strategy underperforms, the gold-set's correct answers must be independent of the strategy being measured. Compounded by the LVT-feedback-loop caveat for one of three queries (corpus-system-prompt overlap from S39b). Recommended S40b shape: (a) human content-fit judgment over retrieval candidates instead of rank-based selection, plus (c) a re-ingested corpus that does not overlap with the agent system prompt. S41 framing at strategic-mode conversation post-S40 close commits the specific shape; the smoke document records the requirement, not the design.

## S39b — Corpus re-ingest and real-corpus discovery-mode authoring
roles: engineer, technical writer
mode: build (verification-and-hygiene bridge between S39 substrate close and S40 runner work)

- Produced: Six commits closed S39b.
  - Commit 1 (`docs(charter)`, 9876bdd) lands the S39b in-flight paragraph at `charter/current-package.md`, captures the migration name-length convention at `log/captures.md` as a project-tooling constraint, and preserves the brief at `briefs/p11/s39b.md`.
  - Commit 2 ingestion-drift commit skipped per Finding 1 — the ingestion CLI runs clean post-S39.
  - Commit 3 (`feat(p11/s39b)`, 1926057) lands forward-only control-plane Alembic migration `0013_lvtguide_reseed` re-seeding LVTGuide with the retrieval tool reference pinned at insertion time and the LVT system prompt lifted verbatim from `briefs/p7/s25.md`. Idempotent; preserves `migration:0013_lvtguide_reseed` actor for provenance symmetry with the seven McKinsey 7-Step roles. One documented mechanical drift: S25 brief named the retrieval strategy "hybrid" citing D66; D66's actual registry name is `parallel_rrf`. Migration uses D66's canonical name.
  - Commit 4 (rename, executed as direct SQL UPDATE; no code commit). Renamed the S39 synthetic gold-set on tenant_a from "P11 retrieval baseline" to "P11 retrieval baseline (synthetic, S39)". Captured as execution evidence in the smoke document; rename is hash-chain-invariant because the canonical revision payload excludes the gold-set name (structurally honest proof recorded in the smoke document's Stage 3).
  - Commit 5 (`fix(p11/s39b)`, 5c7a7f2) corrects ChunkResult attribute access in the gold-set append-entry CLI (`.text` → `.content`, `.score` → `.similarity_score`). Surfaced at S39b stage 4 when discovery-mode authoring ran for the first time; S39's import-shape verification missed this because the typer registration imports cleanly without invoking the candidate-rendering loop. The fix is the methodology demonstration that justifies the bridge-session shape: import-shape verification does not substitute for runtime verification at the discovery-mode CLI altitude.
  - Commit 6 (`docs(p11/s39b)`, f241247) lands the smoke document at `docs/smoke/p11_s39b_corpus_reingest_and_real_authoring.md` with five-stage executed-evidence walkthrough (image rebuild + compose digest pin advancement, LVTGuide re-seed plus chain integrity verification, corpus re-ingest with substitution evidence and one extraction-failure retry path, S39 gold-set rename via SQL UPDATE, real-corpus discovery-mode authoring with the runtime-fix mid-smoke and hash-chain verification byte-identical). Includes four S40 / P12 carryovers explicitly named.
  - Commit 7 (this commit, session log entry).

- Decisions: No new D-entries. Migration name-length convention captured at `log/captures.md` per the brief disposition (vendor-tooling constraint, not architectural decision).

- Tests: 1047 unit tests pass at session close (matches S39 baseline; no test surface changed at S39b). 26 import-linter contracts kept. Tenant-isolation contract harness scenario count unchanged.

- Enforcement layer: No `import-linter` contract changes. No AST enforcement changes. The S39b runtime-fix at commit 5c7a7f2 modifies two attribute references in `apps/cli/_retrieval_evaluation.py`; the existing import-linter contracts continue to police the same cross-context boundaries. The CLI's import-shape verification at S39 passed and still passes; the runtime divergence was below the import-shape surface.

- Operator-decided dispositions at pre-write reconciliation:
  - **Finding 1 (corpus source and path):** Option (c) — substitute corpus drafted at execution time; S25's original synthetic sources never landed in repo and are not recoverable. Operator added shaping note: substitute corpus shape should plausibly answer S39's three queries so the real-corpus gold-set reuses those queries and produces a structural-comparison artefact with the S39 synthetic gold-set at P12. Substitution evidence captured at smoke Stage 2.
  - **Finding 2 (LVTGuide re-seed):** Option (b) — new control-plane migration `0013_lvtguide_reseed`. LVT system prompt lifted verbatim from briefs/p7/s25.md per operator's fidelity ask. One drift surfaced at lift: brief said `retrieval_strategy: hybrid` citing D66; D66's registry names this `parallel_rrf`; mechanical correction at the migration with rationale documented in the migration's docstring. No D-entry required (mechanical rename per D66, not a substantive choice).
  - **Finding 3 (S39 gold-set rename):** Option (a) — direct SQL UPDATE. Structural-honesty observation captured at smoke Stage 3: the rename is hash-chain-invariant because the canonical revision payload at `contexts/retrieval_evaluation/domain/hash_chain.py:70-94` spans only `{revision_number, entries[]}` and explicitly excludes the gold-set name. The S39 gold-set's stored hash `ad94611492299f23b76d7c3eb4602206e88a20223e9ea5983bb0568c43f465a4` still recomputes byte-identically post-rename. This is the structural proof that the rename does not break D109 commitment 4.
  - **Findings 4-6 (autonomous):** Embedding model `nomic-embed-text:v1.5` present on Ollama; dimensions match (768 ↔ 768); per-tenant chunks schema unchanged; discovery-mode CLI runtime verified end-to-end at smoke Stage 4 (with the runtime fix at commit 5).

- methodology (line 1): **Import-shape verification is not runtime verification at the discovery-mode CLI altitude.** S39's smoke document explicitly named that the typer-wiring imports were verified but the `--query` retrieval path was not exercised end-to-end. S39b's stage 4 authoring ran the path for the first time and immediately surfaced an AttributeError on ChunkResult that the import-shape check could not have caught (the typer command's module loads without invoking the candidate-rendering loop, where the bad attribute access lives). The methodology-shape observation worth recording: substrate-session CLI ACs cannot claim closure on import-shape alone; runtime verification against real producers is the closure gate. This is the verification-debt-from-smoke-carryovers pattern the brief named, fired in its first instance at S39b. Recommend P12 audit names this distinctly as "verification-and-hygiene bridge sessions catch CLI runtime divergences that substrate sessions cannot catch with import-shape verification". The bridge-session shape has now produced its first methodology-promoting observation; whether to formally promote depends on whether a second bridge session at P12 or P13 surfaces a similar pattern.

- methodology (line 2): **LVT-query feedback loop in the real-corpus gold-set.** The substitute corpus document `lvt_methodology_overview.md` paraphrases the LVTGuide system prompt being seeded by migration 0013 at the same session. S40's retrieval-evaluation runner against this gold-set will report misleadingly high metric scores on the LVT query because the retrieval corpus and the agent's system prompt share LVT framing content. The artefact is sufficient for the S39b CLI-flow-verification purpose but cannot be a methodologically-clean evaluation baseline. **Carryover to S40 explicitly named:** the methodologically-clean evaluation gold-sets S40 needs require queries whose answers exist in the per-tenant corpus but NOT in the agent's system prompt. The S39b real-corpus gold-set should be marked as "CLI-flow-verification artefact" in any S40 documentation that consumes it.

- methodology (line 3): **CC-autonomously authoring is explicit in the smoke document.** The discovery-mode CLI is interactive at human-operator-time; at this session, CC operates the CLI autonomously and selects "correct" chunk indices from the retrieval candidates without a human in the loop. The smoke document's "CC-autonomously authoring provenance" section names this explicitly so any reader at P12 audit understands that the ground-truth annotations on the real-corpus gold-set reflect CC's rank-based selection, not a human operator's content-fit judgment. Carried as a P12 audit observation.

- methodology (line 4): **Rank-by-similarity ≠ best semantic answer.** Entry 3 illustrates the distinction. Query "what does the bet say about procurement-grade architecture" surfaced ten retrieval candidates with similarity scores. CC selected indices 1, 2, 3 by rank; candidate 4 ("the compliance and architectural constraints") was substantively as relevant or more so than candidate 3 ("the LVT four-levels framing"). A human operator authoring this gold-set with content-fit judgment would likely select 1, 4, 2 instead. The methodology distinction between rank-based and content-fit-based annotation discipline is a P12 audit observation. Recommend the methodologically-clean gold-set at S40 re-authors with content-fit judgment rather than rank-based heuristic.

- methodology (line 5): **Verification-and-hygiene bridge sessions as a session shape.** S39b is Phase 1's first verification-and-hygiene bridge session — distinct from substrate sessions (commit code), feature sessions (commit feature surfaces), and audit sessions (review and consolidate). Trigger condition: substrate session closes with smoke-evidence carryovers that aren't blocking but are real (LVTGuide absent, corpus empty, CLI not runtime-verified). Resolution: a session that resolves the carryovers and tightens the smoke evidence before the next feature session opens. S39b's shape worked: minimal code commits (migration 0013, runtime fix), one substitute-corpus draft, executed-state evidence, runtime divergence caught and fixed mid-smoke. Worth flagging at P12 as a methodology-promoting candidate shape; one instance is observation, recurrence at P12 or P13 would promote.

Five subcommands (`create`, `append-entry`, `list`, `get`, `finalize`) with `append-entry` supporting both interactive prompting and non-interactive `--correct-indices` for smoke scripting; discovery-mode authoring uses `PgVectorSearch` directly per D5/D65.

  - Commit 10 (`docs`, a052857) lands `docs/smoke/p11_s39_retrieval_evaluation_substrate.md` as a five-stage procedural walkthrough plan covering pre-flight migration upgrade, allowlist closure verification on the eight roles including chain integrity recompute, discovery-mode authoring against tenant_a's corpus, finalize with hash-chain integrity verification (recompute via the platform primitive and byte-identical comparison against the stored hash), and list/get for the final view.

- Decisions: D109 landed at commit 1, revised at pre-write reconciliation. Six commitments cover the aggregate root shape, revision value object shape (status + hash-chain fields), entry value object shape (entry_index + expected_chunk_ids ordered array), hash-chain reuse pattern, structural precedent (methodology context, not scoring-sheet), and the five-use-case application layer. Six alternatives considered including (a) methodology inheritance (rejected on cross-context substrate coupling), (c) audit-context refactor with thin extracted helper (committed before pre-write reconciliation surfaced the existing platform primitive; see methodology line 1 below). Kano: must-have on aggregate shape, hash-chain reuse, application-layer lifecycle, methodology-as-precedent framing; performance on use case shape; indifferent on naming conventions.

- Tests: 1047 unit tests pass at session close. 26 new unit tests across `tests/unit/contexts/retrieval_evaluation/` covering domain invariants, canonical-payload sort/render, hash stability across in-memory entry ordering, golden vector pinning the canonical serialisation rule, and full authoring-lifecycle scenarios via in-memory fakes. 26 import-linter contracts kept (one new layers contract for retrieval_evaluation plus four contracts extended with the new context). Tenant-isolation contract harness extends by 13 scenarios; new file `test_retrieval_evaluation_isolation.py` skips cleanly without docker reachable, mirroring run_history precedent.

- Enforcement layer: New `layers-retrieval-evaluation` contract at `.importlinter` enforces the hexagonal layer order (adapters → application → ports → domain). `contexts.retrieval_evaluation.domain` added to `domain-purity` (no Pydantic, no SDK imports, no SQLAlchemy in domain code). `contexts.retrieval_evaluation` added to `litellm-confined`, `langfuse-confined`, `neo4j-confined` (the context has no SDK dependencies beyond SQLAlchemy + asyncpg at the adapter; `fastapi-confined` covers `contexts/` already). Cross-context independence contracts (domain, application, adapters) all extended with the new context. AST enforcement at `tests/_enforcement/test_dockerfile_workspace_coverage.py` requires the new context's pyproject COPY directive in `apps/api/Dockerfile`; landed at commit 3.

- Operator-decided dispositions at pre-write reconciliation:
  - **Finding 1 (role-seed migration shape and location):** Option (a) — new forward-only Alembic migration on the control plane that UPDATEs the eight named roles' allowlists to include the retrieval tool reference + recomputes role-revision hashes per migration 0010's helper pattern. Commit 2 lands this; commit message body explicitly names the hash recompute step (operator's instruction at finding disposition).
  - **Finding 2 (scoring-sheet aggregate divergence):** Option (i) — re-anchor D109 on `contexts/methodology/` as the structural precedent for revision-with-hash-chain; `contexts/evaluation/` is sibling-in-placement only. D105's "sibling-contexts-sharing-patterns" framing rested on a scoring-sheet premise that did not survive reconciliation. D105 not revised at S39; the P12 audit decides whether D105 needs a successor D-entry or whether the gold-set's structural-first status absorbs the correction. See methodology line 2 below.
  - **Finding 3 (hash-chain primitive reuse):** Option (c) — extract `compute_chained_payload_hash` inside `contexts/audit/domain/events.py` as a precursor commit 2.5; commit 3's wrapper would consume it. **This disposition reversed mid-build**; see methodology line 1 below.
  - **Findings 4-8 (autonomous):** Chunk_id is UUID array per ingestion convention; CLI pattern is typer (existing); tenant context flows through `build_tenant_wiring` per the dev-only label-or-UUID convention; composition root wiring at both seams; deferrable FK supported by the existing alembic surface (verified at the migration write at commit 6).

- methodology (line 1): **Mid-build Finding 3 correction — pre-write reconciliation as architectural discovery fires *inside* the build.** Pre-write reconciliation at session open scoped Finding 3 to `contexts/audit/domain/events.py` per the brief's named surface; the Explore agent reported `compute_event_hash` and `GENESIS_HASH` and the operator approved option (c) extract-a-helper. Commit 1 landed D109 with that commitment. Commit 2 then needed to mirror migration 0010's hash recompute pattern, so I read migration 0010 — and the import `from padhanam.security.hash_chain import compute_revision_hash` surfaced. The field-set-agnostic primitive promoted at S24 per D75 already exposes exactly the canonical-JSON-plus-SHA-256-with-chain-link mechanism D109's commit 2.5 would extract. The audit refactor was therefore unnecessary; commit 2.5 did not land. Commit 3's wrapper uses the platform primitive directly. The gold-set context became the third consumer of `padhanam.security.hash_chain.compute_revision_hash` after methodology and role — strengthening D75's second-consumer-promotes pattern observation (now third-consumer-confirms). The structural lesson generalises beyond this instance: pre-write reconciliation at session open scopes its reads against the brief's named surface; if the brief's surface is too narrow (it named the audit context but not the platform helper module that sits at the same architectural altitude), a mid-build reconciliation fires when the *adjacent* file is read for a related reason. The triggering act here was mirroring migration 0010's pattern for commit 2's recompute; this is "the act of using an adjacent piece of code surfaces something the pre-write read missed". Methodology-promotion candidate for the Phase 1 close audit: **mid-build pre-write reconciliation as a distinct sub-pattern** of the broader pre-write-reconciliation-as-architectural-discovery pattern. One instance against a different sub-substrate (within-build vs at-open); awaiting recurrence before P12 audit promotes formally.

- methodology (line 2): **D105's "sibling-contexts-sharing-patterns" framing did not survive S39 pre-write reconciliation.** Finding 2 surfaced that the scoring-sheet aggregate at `contexts/evaluation/` is read-only at S16 with no hash-chain, no status lifecycle, no write use cases. The actual structural precedent for revision-with-hash-chain is `contexts/methodology/` (the methodology and role aggregates carry chain-self-contained revision hashes per D26, even if their lifecycle is migration-time rather than application-time). D109's commitments and reasoning re-anchored on the methodology precedent. The gold-set ships as the first context in Phase 1 with the full create/append/finalize/list/get lifecycle at application-layer granularity with revision-granularity hash-chain. The optimization context at S41 inherits from the gold-set pattern. **D105 is NOT revised at S39.** The P12 audit decides whether D105 needs a successor D-entry or whether the gold-set's structural-first status absorbs the correction; flagging here for the audit per the operator's instruction at Finding 2 disposition.

- methodology (line 3): **Consumer-port-plus-wiring-adapter pattern reaches eleventh and twelfth instances at S39** (`GoldSetRepositoryAdapter`, `GoldSetReaderAdapter` at `apps/cli/_cross_context.py`). The pattern continues to do load-bearing work at every new-context-substrate session; the methodology-document promotion candidate noted at S29b (four reinforcements then) is now at eight more reinforcements (S29b through S39). Strong P12 audit promotion candidate.

- methodology (line 4): **No CLI-side wiring integration test surface exists at `tests/integration/apps/api/`.** The brief at commit 7 said "if no CLI-side wiring test pattern exists, surface the asymmetry in the session log entry as a methodology-candidate observation for the Phase 1 close audit." The asymmetry: the API composition root has the rich integration-test surface (`tests/integration/api/`) that exercises real wiring at request boundaries through the FastAPI app; the CLI composition root has unit tests against the typer CliRunner per `_composition.py`'s test-fixture pattern but no dedicated wiring integration test surface. For the gold-set wiring at S39 the unit-suite + smoke-document combination is sufficient verification; for future wiring additions where the integration-test surface would catch shape mismatches that unit tests miss, the asymmetry is a real gap. Phase 1 close audit input.

- methodology (line 5): **Field-naming inconsistency: gold-set revisions use `previous_event_hash` / `this_event_hash` while methodology and role revisions use `previous_revision_hash` / `this_revision_hash`.** D109 commitment 2 named the audit-mirror convention (`_event_hash`) because the original commitment 4 named the audit-context primitive as the reuse target. The mid-build Finding 3 correction shifted the reuse target to `padhanam.security.hash_chain.compute_revision_hash` (which uses `_revision_hash` internally as the payload key), but the field names on the GoldSetRevision dataclass and the database columns stayed as committed at commit 1 (`_event_hash`). The structural correctness is unaffected — the platform primitive's internal payload-encoding-key is independent of the field names on consumer aggregates — but the cosmetic inconsistency is real. Not changing at S39 to avoid commit-1 churn; the inconsistency is flagged in the gold_set_revision.py docstring and surfaced here for the P12 audit's consideration alongside the D105 framing-versus-as-built question from methodology line 2.
