## S38b — Pre-P11 working-tree hygiene: D107 session-log per-package archival convention, P0-P10 backfill bootstrap, working-tree triage
roles: analyst, architect, technical writer
mode: build (pre-package hygiene)

- Produced: Six commits closed S38b. Commit 1 (`docs(charter)`, 68fa304) landed D107 at `charter/decisions.md` tail committing the cadence shift from per-phase to per-package archival for `log/sessions.md`, with the one-time bootstrap clause covering P0 through P10; same commit edited the Token discipline section at `charter/principles.md` to make the per-package cadence visible at every session-open. Commit 2 (`docs(archive)`, 903da62) backfilled `log/sessions.md` (1879 lines at session open) into per-package files at `docs/archive/sessions/p<n>.md` for n in 1..10; 65 `##` sections distributed per Finding 5's header-naming classification rule with strategic blocks between packages archiving to the prior package's file; live `log/sessions.md` reduced to the 15-line format-preamble header; same commit removed the untracked `docs/archive/sessions/p0-p7.md` combined draft (Finding 3 disposition: split) and the untracked `docs/archive/snapshots/` stale-script-log directory (Finding 4 disposition: delete). Commit 3 (`chore`, b7bcb1c) added the `charter-2026*/` rule to `.gitignore` so the charter-export tooling's dated snapshot directories stay out of `git status`; the matching `.zip` files were already covered by the existing `/*.zip` rule. Commit 4 (`docs(archive)`, b0d8555) committed the 78-line additive correction at `docs/archive/packages/p9.md` capturing the S35a + S35b carryover-hygiene fold-in, harness-integrity restoration, the mid-S35b deliberate-violation-cycle architectural-assumption finding forwarded as P12 audit input, and the Phase 1 substrate-completeness state after P9 plus carryover hygiene (Finding 1 disposition: kept as additive correction with explicit post-archive-append rationale in the commit message). Commit 5 (`docs(charter)`, c0fd3e1) landed `charter/deck.html` as a charter-grade case-study artefact per the D91 brand precedent (Finding 2 disposition: commit to `charter/` as-is). Commit 6 (this commit) landed this session log entry at `log/sessions.md` after the format-preamble header.

- Decisions: D107 lands at S38b (session-log archival cadence shifts to per-package on close, forward from P11 close, with one-time S38b bootstrap covering P0-P10 and a strategic-block-to-prior-package classification rule). No other new D-entries; the four working-tree triage findings are operator-decided dispositions rather than architectural commitments, recorded in commit-message rationale rather than as new D-entries.

- Tests: No test surface touched at S38b (hygiene against working-tree state and documentation surfaces only). Unit-test, contract-test, integration-test, import-linter, and AST-enforcement surfaces unchanged at S38b since the work touches only charter documents, archive files, and `.gitignore`.

- Enforcement layer: No `import-linter` contract changes. No AST enforcement changes. The new D107 convention is enforced socially through CLAUDE.md and `charter/principles.md`'s Token discipline section rather than through tooling; the cadence is reviewed at each package close as part of the close commit. The Acceptance criteria at the S38b brief stand as the bootstrap-event-specific verification surface; future per-package archival events at P11 close onward will follow the same pattern but at smaller scope (one package's worth of sessions per close).

- Operator-decided dispositions at pre-write reconciliation:
  - **Finding 1 (p9.md modification):** Kept as additive correction. The 78-line block captures S35a + S35b carryover-hygiene content that became visible after the original P9 archive landed; the content thematically belongs with P9's retrospective record (S35a/S35b are P9 carryover sessions per session-log headers); append-only spirit preserved (no existing text rewritten or removed). Commit message names the post-archive-append carve-out explicitly.
  - **Finding 2 (charter/deck.html):** Committed to `charter/` as-is. Case-study deck is brand-aligned (uses `charter/brand/tokens.css` palette and typography committed at D91) and sits alongside `bet.md`, `phase-1-prd.md`, and `prfaq.md` as a binding-narrative artefact. The D91 brand-as-charter-grade precedent supports placement here.
  - **Finding 3 (docs/archive/sessions/p0-p7.md):** Split into per-package files. Sourced p1.md through p7.md from `log/sessions.md` per D107's bootstrap clause; superseded untracked combined draft removed in the same commit (commit 2). The combined draft's title "P1 through P7 close sessions" indicated it was a prior strategic-block draft that prepared a combined archive but never landed; the per-package shape is the D107-aligned replacement.
  - **Finding 4 (docs/archive/snapshots/):** Deleted. Directory contents were `.DS_Store` plus a `.last-run.log` (stale charter-export script log from May 11 onward; the referenced zip files were never checked in to that path). Removed as part of commit 2's working-tree cleanup.

- methodology: Reflection prompt 1 — backfill mechanics did surface two small structural surprises in the live session log file worth recording for the Phase 1 close audit. First, the section count came in at 65, not the 51 I'd initially estimated from the section-header grep output. The grep output included the `## Session N (Package P)` format-example line inside the preamble (line 7), so the raw grep count was 66; subtracting the preamble's format example leaves 65 real sections. The under-estimate was a mechanical reading error at finding-classification time, not a structural mis-classification of any section. Worth noting for future archival bootstrap operations: distinguish format-example headers from real section headers explicitly when counting, particularly for files whose format preamble names the section shape with a `##` heading inside a fenced code block (which `grep -c "^## "` still matches because grep does not parse markdown fences). Second, Package P2's session ordering at `log/sessions.md` is S4, S6, S5, S7, S8 — not strictly monotonic, because S6 was committed before S5 at the original write time per the session-log shape (the session-numbering convention was looser at P2). This is a known historical artefact and not a backfill issue, but the per-package archive p2.md now preserves the non-monotonic ordering in line with the source. Surfaced here as a small archival-completeness note rather than as a classification ambiguity.

- methodology (second): Reflection prompt 2 — whether the pre-write reconciliation surfaced any working-tree state that contradicts charter expectations, particularly the `docs/archive/packages/p9.md` modification. The modification did not surface a discipline drift signal under the operator-decided disposition; the additive-correction reading holds because S35a and S35b are P9 carryover sessions per the session-log headers themselves, and the content captures genuine P9 retrospective material that became visible only after the original archive landed. The discipline-question worth recording is narrower: archives are normally append-only at write time, and the 78-line block sat as an in-place modification rather than a clearly-marked seam (no explicit "Post-archive S35a/S35b carryover fold-in" header preceded the block, and the placement is inside the document rather than at the tail). The commit message that lands the modification now names the post-archive-append carve-out explicitly, which retroactively documents the seam. For future post-package-close additions to archive files, the discipline that landed here implicitly is: name the post-archive append in the commit message; preserve append-only spirit in the file content (no rewrite, no remove); place the addition at a thematically coherent seam rather than strictly at the tail when the content thematically completes a preceding section. Whether to promote this to a D-entry or a `charter/methodology.md` line depends on recurrence; one instance is observation, not pattern. The Phase 1 close audit at P12 close is the natural place to revisit if a second post-close archive addition surfaces.

- methodology (third): Reflection prompt 3 — pre-write reconciliation count update and the substrate-extension question. S38b is the nineteenth instance of the pre-write reconciliation practice (S38a was the eighteenth per its session log entry). The substrate-extension question worth recording: prior reconciliation instances were against vendor documentation, file-path facts, interface shapes, and (at S38a) failure-mode causal structure. S38b's reconciliation was against working-tree state — modified-tracked-files, untracked-files, ignored-state, and the contents of dated scratch directories at the repo root. Working-tree state is a different reconciliation substrate from the prior instances because the canonical-state surface is `git status` plus actual file contents rather than vendor docs or test failure output. The pattern itself is identical (read the actual state, surface findings, pause for operator disposition on the decision-dependent items, proceed autonomously on the unambiguous items). The novelty is the substrate, not the mechanism. Per the captures-candidate threshold (don't propose candidates for "existing methodology applied correctly" without structural novelty or 3x recurrence), this is one instance against a different substrate — observation rather than promotion candidate. The Phase 1 close audit at P12 close is the natural place to consolidate the substrate-class taxonomy if more substrate variants surface (e.g., reconciling against external-system state, against historical-commit state, against tooling-output state).

- methodology (fourth): The S38b bootstrap operation itself is worth a brief methodology note. The brief structured the work as one charter commit, one archive backfill commit, one gitignore commit, then triage commits per the operator-decided dispositions. The shape held cleanly — each commit's diff is interpretable in isolation; the audit trail at `git log --oneline` reads as a coherent sequence of cadence-shift, content-relocation, scratch-hide, and triage commits. The pattern is reusable for future bootstrap-style operations (cadence shifts, convention adoptions, archival reorganisations): one commit lands the convention with its D-entry; one or more commits land the content moves that the convention requires; one commit handles working-tree scratch hygiene; one-commit-per-disposition handles operator-decided working-tree triage. The order matters because the convention commit makes the content-move commit interpretable in audit, and the triage commits are independent leaves that can land in any order after the convention is committed. Worth noting for future S-prefix hygiene sessions whose deliverable is a cadence shift or convention adoption.

## S49 — Post-S48 hygiene: methodology promotions and captures formalizations
roles: analyst, PM, technical writer
mode: strategic-mode hygiene, executing briefs/p13/s49.md (charter-only; two commits)

S49 closed the post-S48 hygiene discipline-altitude work between the S48a/S48b parallel-thread close and the resolver-disambiguation build session opening. Two commits landed without any code, test, or D-entry surfaces: commit 1 promoted three methodology candidates accumulated across the post-S46 architectural arc to `charter/methodology.md`'s Patterns observed section (interface-versus-implementation discipline at seven instances across three surfaces; component-quality-versus-integration-smoke discipline at two-instance evidence plus first structural implementation D137; structural-test SSOT binding at two-instance evidence) plus appended PROMOTED resolution lines to the three contributing captures entries (append-only per the audit-trail-integrity discipline). Commit 2 appended two new captures entries (scope-discipline-at-brief-authoring at first-instance observation; gold-set authoring plus confidence-aware composition connection observation) plus this S49 session-log entry plus the S49 close marker at `charter/current-package.md`. The two-commit shape separates the methodology-document load-bearing promotions from the forward-looking captures formalizations for clean audit trail.

- Produced: Two commits. (1) `docs(charter): promote three disciplines to methodology.md from S47-S48b evidence` (da56b0e) — three new sub-sections under Patterns observed at `charter/methodology.md` (interface-versus-implementation, component-quality-versus-integration-smoke, structural-test SSOT binding) plus three PROMOTED resolution lines appended to the contributing captures entries at `log/captures.md` (2026-05-25 [S47/UX convergence] interface-versus-implementation; 2026-05-26 [S48b] component-quality; 2026-05-26 [S48b] structural-test SSOT binding). (2) `docs(captures): formalize scope-discipline and gold-set/confidence observations` (this commit) — two new captures entries appended to `log/captures.md` (2026-05-26 [S48b framing] scope-discipline drift at brief authoring with the pattern-family observation alongside interface-versus-implementation; 2026-05-26 [S48b reflection 2] gold-set authoring plus confidence-aware composition with forward-relevance bound to D137, D110, and D134 extensions) plus this session-log entry plus the S49 close marker at `charter/current-package.md`.

- Decisions: No new D-entries; three methodology promotions to `charter/methodology.md`; three captures-entry resolution-line updates at `log/captures.md`; two captures formalizations at `log/captures.md`.

- Tests: No change. 1776 unit + contract tests hold from S48b close baseline; 33/33 import-linter contracts kept; AST enforcement green. Charter-only session; no test surface touched.

- Reflection (methodology-entry composition under Patterns observed, prompt 1): The three new sub-sections read as distinct patterns when placed adjacent in the section. Interface-versus-implementation is about committing implementations when the architecture should commit interfaces; component-quality-versus-integration-smoke is about using the wrong evaluation surface for component-quality questions; structural-test SSOT binding is about discipline commitments that admit structural tests landing those tests at the commitment's commit. The three pattern bodies inhabit different altitudes (framing-altitude drafting discipline; evaluation-surface selection discipline; commitment-binding discipline) and reference different mechanisms (interface-versus-implementation question; port-and-adapter substrate; structural test surfaces). No revision needed at draft time; the cross-pattern non-overlap pre-write reconciliation surface 3 closed cleanly.

- Reflection (pattern-density observation closure, prompt 2): The interface-versus-implementation entry's pattern-density paragraph carries the observation and its resolution at load-bearing weight without restating the convergence-conversation history in detail. The seven-instance enumeration carries the evidence; the pattern-density paragraph names the open question the five-instance captures entry posed (whether strategic-mode altitude inherently produces the drift or whether the convergence's particular shape produced it) and answers it (the seven instances across three surfaces close the question: strategic-mode altitude predictably produces the drift; the discipline binds at every drafting surface when explicitly named as a reconciliation surface). The load-bearing-versus-historical balance is appropriate; the methodology entry serves forward-relevance more than retrospective narration.

- Reflection (captures-entry resolution shape, prompt 3): The three resolution-line updates appended PROMOTED bullets to the original entries rather than replacing the prior resolution text. This composes cleanly with future recurrence accumulation: subsequent instances of the same pattern can land at the captures entry's recurrence log (as a new appended observation or as a sub-bullet under the existing entry) without contradicting the resolution; the PROMOTED line names the methodology document's forward-relevance discipline as the binding mechanism for future recurrence. The pattern matches the existing convention for entries with multiple resolution lines (the qwen2.5:14b entry at line 670 carries `resolution (initial)` and `resolution (S48a close, 2026-05-26)`; the S49 entries follow the same `resolution (S49, 2026-05-26)` shape).

- Reflection (forward-relevance bindings, prompt 4): **Substantive paragraph because the forward-relevance bindings vary in operational concreteness.** The interface-versus-implementation entry's forward-relevance is the most concrete: pre-write reconciliation surfaces at session prompts include an explicit interface-versus-implementation check from S49 forward; the discipline composes with the existing path-naming discipline (S43b) and the build-at-second-instance discipline (D127 alternative (d)) as the three pre-write reconciliation disciplines that catch the most common strategic-mode drafting drift classes. P14 epic framing inherits this directly. The component-quality entry's forward-relevance names four specific future component-quality substrates (prompt-revision, structured-output reliability per class, retrieval-strategy quality, confidence-calibration) with their activation triggers; each future substrate inherits the D137 shape (bounded context or substrate-altitude, gold set, metric calculator, audit chain integration, CLI). The structural-test SSOT binding entry's forward-relevance names five structural-test surfaces (AST-pattern detection, symbol-identity assertions, import-linter contracts, file-topology budget assertions, path-naming verification) and three future commitment classes that admit structural tests (D136 multi-channel UX primitives at activation triggers; future ConversationFlow implementers at P14+; future gold-set authoring disciplines per the captures observation from the same session). The structural-test entry's forward-relevance leans on the activation-trigger pattern more heavily than the other two; this is appropriate because the entry's discipline is about *evaluation at draft time* (does the commitment admit a structural test?) rather than about a specific test surface, so the forward-relevance must enumerate the surfaces and the commitment classes rather than commit a specific Phase 2-A test. All three forward-relevance bindings are operationally concrete enough to act on at the relevant future surfaces.

- Reflection (scope-discipline-at-brief-authoring recurrence test naming, prompt 5): The captures entry names the recurrence test at the next strategic-mode brief-drafting moment that proposes substantive substrate work, with concrete test surfaces (the next P14 ConversationFlow implementer brief or the next post-resolver-disambiguation build session brief). The test fires if a future brief commits maximal scope by precedent rather than testing each piece against Phase-current YAGNI; the test passes if the brief commits only the load-bearing minimum with deferrals named at activation triggers (the S48b Option B shape). The recurrence test is concrete enough to detect at the next strategic-mode brief-drafting moment.

- Reflection (gold-set-plus-confidence composition reflection, prompt 6): The captures entry is the right home for the observation at first-instance. The connection between gold-set authoring discipline and D134's confidence-aware composition primitive is structurally significant because the substrate at D137 measures model behaviour against gold-set expectations, but production behaviour at the same input class consumes D134's port; if gold-set authoring treats ambiguity as model failure where D134 treats it as architectural opportunity, the substrate's signal and the production cell's behaviour diverge on the same class of input. The forward-relevance binding names extensions at D137, D110, and D134; future evaluation substrates or D134 extensions inherit the connection as captures-entry reference rather than requiring D-entry-level prose changes at the current sessions. If recurrence at gold-set-authoring or D134-extension surfaces strengthens the observation, second-instance promotion to `charter/methodology.md` would warrant updating D134's prose or D137's prose with explicit forward-references to the methodology entry; first-instance does not.

- Reflection (methodology surfaces, prompt 7): One new methodology candidate surfaced at S49 itself — the **shape-of-hygiene-session** observation. S49 is the second hygiene-session-shape instance in P13 (after the pre-S45 principles assessment / pre-S45 Revisable harness hygiene block plus pre-S44 hygiene), but the first that is purely charter-only (no code; no tests; no enforcement layers) and sized at exactly two commits with a clear two-commit shape (load-bearing-promotions + forward-looking-formalizations-plus-close). The discipline of "charter-only sessions sized at one to two commits with methodology promotions and captures formalizations as the two commit-shape components" may be promotion-candidate if a second instance lands at the next hygiene session (post-P13 close hygiene or pre-P14 hygiene). Not captured as a new captures entry at S49 because the second-instance test is genuinely open (the next hygiene session may take a different shape if it has substantive code workitems); recorded here in the methodology line for future-session inheritance.

methodology: Three Patterns observed promotions land at `charter/methodology.md` (interface-versus-implementation discipline at seven-instance evidence across three surfaces; component-quality-versus-integration-smoke discipline at two-instance evidence plus first structural implementation D137; structural-test SSOT binding at two-instance evidence). Two captures formalizations land at `log/captures.md` (scope-discipline-at-brief-authoring at first instance with the pattern-family connection to interface-versus-implementation; gold-set authoring plus confidence-aware composition at first instance with forward-relevance bound to D137, D110, and D134 extensions). ## S50 — Resolver disambiguation on duplicate-title cases
roles: engineer, architect, technical writer
mode: build session, executing briefs/p13/s50.md (two commits: feat + docs)

S50 closed the S48a forward finding on resolver behaviour when multiple cases share the same title via cell-layer extension. The architectural disposition committed at the captures entry: resolution-ambiguity (high-confidence intent + multi-match resolve_target) routes to D134's existing Case 2 shape-aware clarification surface, not to a new fourth case or a separate disambiguation path. PendingClarification accommodates the sub-case via the existing `proposed_intent: dict[str, Any]` field carrying a `resolution_candidates` sidecar that `parse_intent` ignores; operator's positional reply ("1", "2", "3") resolves the pending and executes the original action against the chosen case via a `_dispatch_proceed_against_resolved` short-circuit. The PortfolioGateway.CaseSummary DTO extends with three discriminator fields (created_at, last_activity_at, data_point_count) computed eagerly at `find_cases` for Phase 2-A scale; TargetCandidate carries optional discriminators forward through ResolutionOutcome.candidates; the cell formats raw datetimes/ints to short human strings (relative-time helper with absolute-date fallback beyond 30 days) per the channel-decides-format pattern at D135.

- Produced: Two commits. (1) `feat(p13/s50): resolver multi-match with disambiguating-signal rendering` (a05f7fe) touching seven files across the four hexagonal layers — port DTO extension at `contexts/messaging/application/ports/portfolio_gateway.py`; TargetCandidate.discriminators + ResolutionOutcome.candidates at `contexts/messaging/application/resolve_target.py`; adapter eager enrichment via `get_case_detail` at `apps/api/_portfolio_gateway_wiring.py`; cell helpers and dispatch paths at `contexts/messaging/application/manual_entry_cell.py` (relative-time formatter; discriminator composition; positional-reply parser; numbered candidate renderer; `_dispatch_resolution_clarify_with_pending` for the multi-match dispatch; `_handle_resolution_selection` for the positional-selection branch; `_dispatch_proceed_against_resolved` for the id-hint short-circuit; positional-reply branch in `turn()` between active-pending check and confirm/cancel classification); 11 new unit tests across `test_resolve_target.py` (2: AMBIGUOUS carries candidates with discriminators; TargetCandidate.discriminators defaults empty) and `test_manual_entry_cell.py` (9: 4 helper tests; 5 cell-flow tests covering multi-match → pending creation, positional-selection executes against chosen id, out-of-range re-renders without resolving, correcting reply cancels the resolution pending, NO_MATCH keeps simpler clarification path). (2) `docs(p13/s50): live-stack smoke verifying resolver disambiguation` (this commit) landing the procedural smoke document at `docs/smoke/p13_s50_resolver_disambiguation.md` (five-stage walk against tenant_a's three accumulated "Q3 portfolio review" cases), the captures entry at `log/captures.md` recording the resolution-ambiguity disposition with the P14 second-instance recurrence test named, the S50 brief preservation at `briefs/p13/s50.md` per D43, this session-log entry, and the S50 close marker at `charter/current-package.md`.

- Decisions: No new D-entries; one captures-entry formalisation recording the resolution-ambiguity-routes-to-D134-shape-aware-clarification disposition with the P14 second-instance trigger named for explicit architecture.md prose addition. The disposition operates within D134's existing three-case discipline as a sub-case of Case 2; the cell-layer extension is application of an existing primitive rather than a new architectural commitment.

- Tests: 1787 unit + contract tests pass (+11 from S49/S48b baseline of 1776: 2 in test_resolve_target.py for the new candidates shape and discriminator carry-through; 9 in test_manual_entry_cell.py for helpers, multi-match dispatch, positional selection, out-of-range re-render, correcting cancel, no-match clarification). 33 of 33 import-linter contracts kept (no new contracts; no contract changes — the extension stays within the existing layers-messaging hexagonal contract). AST enforcement green. No Alembic migration at S50 (PendingClarification schema accommodates the sub-case via the existing dict-typed field).

- Reflection (D134 sub-case accommodation, prompt 1): The pre-write reconciliation Surface 3 hypothesis held cleanly. PendingClarification's `proposed_intent: dict[str, Any]` field tolerates the `resolution_candidates` sidecar key without `parse_intent` rejection (verified by reading `parse_intent` at `contexts/messaging/domain/intent.py:152-188` — it uses `raw.get("intent_type", "")` and reads specific known fields, ignoring unrecognised keys). `proposed_action_summary` accommodates the ambiguity phrasing. No schema change required. The build-at-second-instance discipline closed on the right side: extending the entity now would have built substrate for a single use case before evidence accumulates that other ConversationFlow implementers need a different sidecar shape.

- Reflection (discriminator computation surface, prompt 2): The trade-off landed at eager computation in `find_cases` (the adapter calls `get_case_detail` per case). Cost analysis at framing: ~50-200 ms across N case-detail loads at Phase 2-A scale (≤5 cases per tenant during dogfooding), well under the LLM call's 1.5-3 s baseline. The lazy alternative (compute only on AMBIGUOUS via a new enrich-by-id port method) would have added a port method and an extra adapter pass at the AMBIGUOUS path; the gain (N+1 reduction in find_cases path) does not materialise at Phase 2-A scale. Phase 2-B+ swap to lazy enrichment activates when tenant case counts grow; the adapter is the single change site, no cell-layer or port-shape change required. The choice composes with the existing `find_data_points` adapter pattern which already does N case-detail loads without optimisation.

- Reflection (operator reply pattern coverage, prompt 3): Positional-only as confirmed at framing. The cell handles bare positive integers ("1", "2", "3", "12", etc.) — including whitespace-padding and trailing punctuation per the `_normalise` helper. Descriptive forms ("the older one", "the new one", "the empty one") deferred to second-instance need at P14 or operator-dogfooding signal. The positional parser scope is narrow (whole-message integer only — not "1 please" or "first one") to avoid collision with fresh-turn replies that legitimately start with a digit; the trade-off costs operator friction at the edge (operator must reply with just the number) but eliminates a class of false-positive selections.

- Reflection (first operational application of S49's interface-versus-implementation discipline, prompt 4): **Substantive paragraph because the discipline caught two latent shape decisions at draft time and the methodology evidence is load-bearing.** S50 is the first session from S49 forward to exercise the standing pre-write reconciliation Surface 6. The discipline fired twice productively: (a) at the CaseSummary DTO extension — the interface commitment is "the case-lookup port returns multi-match results with disambiguating signals" (CaseSummary gains discriminator fields); the implementation behind it is the specific signals chosen (`created_at`, `last_activity_at`, `data_point_count`). Future Phase 2-B+ signals (e.g., `case_priority`, `tenant_owner_last_referenced`) extend the DTO without breaking the resolution path; the interface stays stable; (b) at the PendingClarification reuse — the interface commitment is "proposed_intent is the cell's structured-intent envelope at pending creation and re-parse-able at resolution"; the implementation behind it is the specific dict shape (which key carries `resolution_candidates`). Any future Phase 2-B+ resolution-disambiguation surface that needs richer ambiguity context (e.g., for DataPoint resolution-ambiguity, or for cross-context resolution-ambiguity at P14 implementers) extends the dict shape without changing the entity schema. The discipline operationally proved its forward-relevance binding from S49 commit 1 — both shape decisions were committed at the interface altitude with implementation choices explicitly separable. Forward methodology evidence: the standing pre-write reconciliation surface binds productively at the framing-time check; the discipline's recurrence test from S49 closes positively at first instance.

- Reflection (resolution-ambiguity first-instance shape, prompt 5): The captures entry frames the disposition as convention-altitude at one instance with P14 second-instance triggering architecture.md prose addition. S50 did not surface any architectural-altitude observation that would justify committing the disposition at architecture.md now. The disposition lives within D134's three-case discipline as a sub-case of Case 2; the cell-layer extension is application of an existing primitive. If P14 audit-conversation or mirror-conversation implementers face the same sub-case at their resolution surfaces, the cross-implementer commitment language at architecture.md becomes load-bearing; until then, the captures entry is the right home. Build-at-second-instance discipline (D127 alternative (d), promoted as part of S49's three pre-write reconciliation disciplines) binds here.

- Reflection (methodology surfaces, prompt 6): **Substantive paragraph because two methodology candidates surface at S50 worth recording.** First, the **first operational application of S49's interface-versus-implementation discipline** is forward methodology evidence the discipline binds at the framing-time pre-write reconciliation surface as the S49 promotion forecast. Caught two latent shape decisions at draft time (CaseSummary DTO discriminator-field extension as interface; PendingClarification dict shape as interface) before code drafts. The discipline composes cleanly with the existing path-naming and build-at-second-instance disciplines as the three standing pre-write reconciliation surfaces. Second, the operator surfaced a **sub-shape of the scope-discipline-at-brief-authoring pattern** at S50 framing — the brief left reply-pattern coverage as "framing's choice" rather than committing the load-bearing minimum at brief drafting. Neither the maximal-by-precedent drift the S48b first instance recorded nor the load-bearing-minimum the discipline aims at; this is a third mode, functionally similar to maximal-by-precedent because both push the scope-narrowing work to framing reconciliation rather than handling it at the brief. The S49 captures entry (formalized at S49 commit 2) may want refinement at next hygiene to acknowledge the three modes: load-bearing-minimum-committed (target shape); maximal-by-precedent (S48b first instance); deferred-to-framing (S50 sub-shape). Both observations forward to the next hygiene methodology review.

methodology: First operational application of S49's interface-versus-implementation discipline as a standing pre-write reconciliation surface; the discipline caught two latent shape decisions at draft time (CaseSummary DTO discriminator-field extension committed as interface; PendingClarification dict shape committed as interface). Resolution-ambiguity disposition recorded at captures with P14 second-instance trigger named for architecture.md prose addition per the build-at-second-instance discipline. A sub-shape of scope-discipline-at-brief-authoring surfaced at S50 framing (deferred-to-framing mode alongside the maximal-by-precedent mode S48b first instance recorded); the S49 captures-entry formalization may want refinement at next hygiene to acknowledge the three modes (load-bearing-minimum-committed; maximal-by-precedent; deferred-to-framing).

## S51 — P14 opens: CitedResponse Protocol + ArtefactCitation + audit-conversation ConversationFlow implementer
roles: engineer, architect, technical writer
mode: build session (substrate + first implementer), executing briefs/p14/s51.md (seven commits — the brief's eight collapsed at Finding 1)

S51 opened P14 with the CitedResponse Protocol substrate at shared_kernel plus the audit-conversation ConversationFlow implementer end-to-end. Pre-write reconciliation across five framing-altitude surfaces resolved five structural-honesty findings before code drafting (four at framing close, one at build-mode pre-write reconciliation): (1) the brief's new AuditQueryPort + Postgres adapter + Alembic migration drop because the existing AuditEventReader from S36 covers the seven filter dimensions plus cursor pagination plus chain-integrity verification; S51 collapsed from eight to seven commits. (2) The brief's `shared_kernel/conversation_flow/` directory split collapses to single-file `shared_kernel/conversation_flow.py` per the codebase convention (intent_classification.py, structured_output.py, inference.py, et al. all single files). (3) The audit-conversation gold set lives at `tests/fixtures/intent_classification/audit_conversation_gold_set.yaml` per the existing fixture path convention with INTENT_CLASSES extended via tuple addition (10 classes covering both manual_entry and audit_conversation surfaces); the D137 substrate parameterisation activation trigger captured at deferred-decisions for the third-or-later surface promotion. (4) ArtefactCitation does not exist anywhere in the codebase (the brief's "currently at the manual entry cell module" framing was structurally false), and the operator selected the symmetric-with-mirror heterogeneous-citations shape on architectural-symmetry-between-co-implementers plus render-layer-decoupling plus refactor-consolidation grounds (CellResponse refactored at S51 commit 2 rather than deferring an expanded refactor to S52). (5) The brief's "dispatch port routes audit queries to the audit-conversation cell" presupposed dispatch-decision logic that does not exist (the Twilio webhook always dispatches to manual_entry_cell; no cell-selection mechanism). Disposition deferred dispatch routing to S52 framing when the three-cell topology forces the question to be answered cleanly; S51 wires the cell at the context layer with contract-harness registration plus a script-driven smoke.

- Produced: Seven commits. (1) `docs(charter): D138 + D139 + architecture.md + packages.md + deferred-decisions.md + p14-epic.md + captures.md for P14 framing (with S51 reconciliations)` (e279f56) — charter touch-points landing D138 (CitedResponse Protocol at single-file shared_kernel; ArtefactCitation typed value object with discriminator) plus D139 (resolution-ambiguity cross-cutting promotion of S50's pattern to all ConversationFlow implementers) plus architecture.md additions (Protocol sub-section, resolution-ambiguity sub-section, audit-conversation context note consuming existing AuditEventReader, mirror-conversation forward-looking + drill-down stateless-per-turn sub-sections) plus packages.md P14 line revision (narrow-scope to two ConversationFlow implementers) plus deferred-decisions.md updates (close shared-kernel CitedResponse entry by D138, confirm multi-channel UX no-P14-activation, add three P14 framing deferrals: calendar/email-read at P15+, mirror-conversation drill-down state entity, D137 substrate parameterisation) plus new charter/packages/p14-epic.md file plus five captures entries (S49 three-modes refinement, interface-vs-implementation operational evidence carryforward, mirror-conversation drill-down state-entity activation trigger, S46 cited_audit_events empty-field gap closure, substrate-inheritance survey methodology candidate at three-instance evidence promotion-ready) plus current-package.md S51 in-flight marker plus the brief preserved at briefs/p14/s51.md. (2) `feat(shared_kernel): CitedResponse Protocol + ArtefactCitation; refactor CellResponse to satisfy Protocol` (4541e4c) — CitedResponse runtime-checkable Protocol + ArtefactCitation typed value object at single-file shared_kernel/conversation_flow.py alongside the existing ConversationFlow Protocol; CellResponse refactored at contexts/messaging/application/cell_response.py to carry cited_artefacts: tuple[ArtefactCitation, ...]; five cite sites at manual_entry_cell.py wrap UUIDs with ArtefactCitation; render layer calls _short_hex(a.artefact_id); two unit-test assertions updated; 4 new shared_kernel unit tests (construction + freeze + discriminator rejection + isinstance conforming/non-conforming). (3) `feat(audit_conversation): cell + intent classification + response + resolution-ambiguity routing` (7e0e6c5) — new bounded context at contexts/audit_conversation/ with domain (six intent VOs, parse_audit_intent), application (cell, response, query_builder, ports/portfolio_case_lookup); audit-classification prompt+schema at shared_kernel/intent_classification_audit.py; contexts/messaging/api.py created as cross-context facade exposing PendingClarification machinery for audit-conversation's consumption; .importlinter extended with layers-audit-conversation contract plus messaging.api ignore_imports; 31 new unit tests (intent VOs, query builder, response, cell happy paths plus resolution-ambiguity). (4) `feat(audit_conversation): WhatsApp render extension for AuditConversationResponse` (0331603) — render_for_whatsapp at contexts/audit_conversation/application/response.py with Shape-1 citation line plus 2 new render tests. (5) `test(contract): CitedResponse conformance + resolution-ambiguity conformance + audit-conversation harness registration` (b6157d2) — two new conformance scenarios at tests/contract/conversation_flow/ (CitedResponse Protocol satisfiability for every registered implementer; resolution-ambiguity routing through D134's PendingClarification per D139) plus the audit-conversation harness registration module; both implementers (manual_entry_cell, audit_conversation_cell) pass both scenarios. (6) `feat(intent_classification_evaluation): audit-conversation gold set + INTENT_CLASSES extension + runner parameterisation` (f8b186a) — INTENT_CLASSES tuple extended to 10 (4 manual_entry + 6 audit_conversation), INTENT_SURFACES tuple and DEFAULT_INTENT_SURFACE added; IntentClassificationGoldSet gains intent_surface field defaulting to manual_entry; YamlGoldSetReader reads intent_surface; the runner gains a per-surface (prompt_builder, schema, result_key) lookup table; audit_conversation_gold_set.yaml authored at tests/fixtures/intent_classification/ with 24 entries; 8 new unit tests (6 domain + 2 YAML loader). (7) `docs(p14/s51): procedural smoke + session log + current-package transition` (this commit) — procedural smoke document at docs/smoke/p14_s51_audit_conversation.md walking 11 stages end-to-end against tenant_a, this session-log entry, and the current-package.md S51 close marker.

- Decisions: Two new D-entries committed at commit 1 (D138 CitedResponse Protocol; D139 resolution-ambiguity cross-cutting routing). No additional D-entries across commits 2-7.

- Tests: 1672 unit + contract tests pass (+54 from S50 baseline of 1618 at the freshly-rebuilt python 3.14.3 / pytest 9.0.3 environment; baseline differs from the S50 quoted 1787 due to environment-level test collection differences captured at commit 2 verification — no test regressions). 34 of 34 import-linter contracts kept (+1 layers-audit-conversation; messaging.api → messaging.{domain, application} ignores added). AST enforcement green. No Alembic migration at S51 (audit-conversation consumes existing reader; CitedResponse Protocol is shared_kernel-only).

- Reflection (CitedResponse Protocol second-instance closure, prompt 1): The Protocol shape committed at framing held against AuditConversationResponse end-to-end. The three citation tuple fields (cited_intake_records, cited_audit_events, cited_artefacts) populated naturally at the cell's response composition path. The build-at-second-instance discipline closed cleanly on the architecture-symmetry side: ArtefactCitation discriminator load-bearing at S51 because audit-conversation surfaces heterogeneous citations (Case + DataPoint from cited audit events' resource_type field). No Protocol-shape revision surfaced. The single-file shared_kernel placement per Finding 2 disposition reads cleanly with both the existing ConversationFlow Protocol and the new CitedResponse Protocol cohabiting; the codebase-convention reading inherited the existing single-file pattern for sibling primitives.

- Reflection (resolution-ambiguity cross-cutting promotion, prompt 2): S50's first-instance pattern (manual entry cell duplicate-title case resolution) generalised cleanly to audit-conversation's title-ambiguous case references. Both implementers now share: the PendingClarification consumer port surface; the proposed_intent dict shape with resolution_candidates sidecar; the positional-integer reply parser; the candidate-list rendering pattern. The candidate-list shape at audit-conversation differs from the manual entry cell's: audit-conversation cites candidates via ArtefactCitation (heterogeneous discriminator surfaces) while the manual entry cell renders candidates with creation-time / last-activity / data-point-count discriminators. The interaction shape is identical (numbered list + positional-reply); the structural enforcement at the contract harness scenario (commit 5) verifies both implementers route via PendingClarification with the resolution_candidates sidecar. The shape variance is structurally honest: each implementer's candidate context (the discriminating signal the operator uses to pick) is implementer-specific while the routing-through-D134-PendingClarification contract is cross-cutting.

- Reflection (AuditEventReader tenant-scoping discipline, prompt 3): Tenant-scoping flows through the existing AuditEventReader port boundary structurally: every list_audit_events_with_filters call requires tenant_context: TenantContext | None; the per_tenant destination raises AuditQueryRoutingError when tenant_context is None. Audit-conversation always passes destination='per_tenant' plus actor.tenant_context. The existing tests/contract/tenant_isolation/test_audit_reader_isolation.py from S36 covers the discipline. No new tenant-isolation contract scenario authored at S51 — the audit-conversation cell inherits the discipline structurally. No code paths where tenant_id could be bypassed surfaced at integration testing (the contract harness scenarios construct the cell with a tenant-scoped actor; the cell threads actor.tenant_context through every reader call).

- Reflection (audit-event citation closure of S46's empty-field gap, prompt 4): cited_audit_events populated naturally at audit-conversation as expected. The reader's AuditEventListPage returns events tuple where each AuditEventRecord carries its id field; the cell maps `(event.id for event in page.events)` into the tuple. The composition is one-line; no friction. cited_artefacts heterogeneous population required slightly more code (deduplicating by (resource_type, resource_id) so the same case appearing in multiple events cites once; handling the UUID parse for resource_id which may be non-UUID-shaped for some resource_types; restricting to "case" and "data_point" discriminator values per the Phase 2-A union). No composition friction beyond standard. The S46 empty-field gap closes structurally on the read-side without write-result DTO extension at intake.

- Reflection (interface-versus-implementation discipline at S51, prompt 5): S50 first exercised the discipline at standing surface (two findings caught at draft time: CaseSummary DTO discriminator-field; PendingClarification dict shape). S51 is the second exercise. Three interface decisions caught at brief-altitude pre-write reconciliation (Findings 1, 2, 4) plus one caught at build-mode pre-write reconciliation (Finding 5). The discipline operationally enforced across both surfaces. Specifically: (a) Finding 1 (AuditEventReader vs new port) is interface-vs-implementation: the interface commitment is "audit-conversation reads audit events through a read port"; the implementation behind it is which specific port (existing reader vs new port). The brief committed an implementation; the framing-altitude reconciliation caught the implementation as a duplication of an existing interface. (b) Finding 4 (ArtefactCitation existence + symmetric-with-mirror shape) is interface-vs-implementation at multiple altitudes: the interface commitment is "cited_artefacts surfaces heterogeneous artefact citations"; the implementation behind it is whether the discriminator lands at S51 vs S52, and whether ArtefactCitation is authored fresh or moved. The brief committed an implementation (move from manual entry cell) based on a false codebase assumption; the framing-altitude reconciliation caught the assumption and the implementation question separately. (c) Finding 5 (dispatch routing) is interface-vs-implementation at the cross-cell-routing altitude: the interface commitment is "the platform dispatches inbound messages to the correct ConversationFlow implementer"; the implementation behind it is HOW (meta-classifier, prefix-based, message-shape-based). The brief presupposed an implementation; the build-mode reconciliation caught the implementation question as load-bearing at S52 not S51.

- Reflection (component-quality versus integration-smoke discipline, prompt 6): The audit-conversation gold set at commit 6 and the procedural smoke at commit 7 stayed distinct surfaces verifying distinct properties. The gold set evaluates classifier accuracy in isolation against the structured-output port (no Postgres read, no audit reader, no cell composition); the smoke evaluates end-to-end behaviour including AuditEventReader query execution, PendingClarification lifecycle, channel-adapter rendering, and audit-chain integrity. The boundary held cleanly. Component-quality verdict at S51 close pending operator execution of the smoke against tenant_a; the gold-set evaluation is reproducible against the YAML fixture without operator action.

- Reflection (gold-set-authoring plus confidence-aware composition recurrence test, prompt 7): At audit-conversation gold-set authoring (commit 6), the operator-shaped inputs were chosen to exercise each AuditIntentType class cleanly. The 24-entry fixture's authoring did not surface ambiguity-at-the-input-class altitude (operator-shaped phrasings map to single intent classes per the gold-set entry; no entry is structurally ambiguous between two classes). One entry pair carries a subtle ambiguity: "show all case creations" (find_by_event_type) versus "what case creates happened today by operator?" (find_by_combination) — the former tests event-type alone, the latter tests combination. If a model classifies the former as find_by_combination (with the operator implicit), the classification is structurally defensible; the gold's expected find_by_event_type would call it wrong. This is the S48b reflection-2 recurrence test surfacing: legitimate ambiguity is a structural property of the input, not a model failure. Future extension at D137 alternative (c) revision-lifecycle gold sets can record alternative-acceptable classifications per the captures entry's third option. For S51 the gold-set entries hold as-authored; the smoke at Stage 10 records any operator-observed ambiguity per the captures entry's recurrence test commitment.

- Reflection (scope-discipline-at-brief-authoring three-modes recurrence test, prompt 8): The P14 framing committed at brief-altitude all three modes (load-bearing-minimum-committed via narrow-scope on audit + mirror; deferred-to-framing on mirror drill-down state; rejected-by-precedent on calendar/email-read pushed to P15+). The S51 brief's execution stayed within the load-bearing minimum at most surfaces, but Finding 5 (dispatch routing) surfaced a brief-altitude commitment that drifted toward maximal-by-precedent (the brief presupposed the dispatch port routes audit queries to audit-conversation when in fact the dispatch port runs ONE cell and the webhook always dispatches to manual_entry_cell). The build-mode reconciliation caught the drift and reshaped to option (c) defer-to-S52. The three-modes framing operationally held — the drift was caught and reshaped — but the build-mode catch (rather than framing-mode catch) is a new evidence point: brief-altitude scope discipline doesn't always catch every drift; build-mode pre-write reconciliation supplements framing-mode at exactly the surface where dispatch-shape assumptions bridge from one surface (webhook routing) to another (cell dispatch). The fourth-instance evidence at the substrate-inheritance survey methodology candidate captured that surface bridging explicitly.

The substrate-inheritance survey methodology candidate carries forward at four-instance evidence base (three at framing + one at build-mode); promotion candidacy strong for the next hygiene session. Interface-versus-implementation discipline at S49 standing surface bound productively across multiple findings, exercising the second-recurrence test cleanly. ## S52 — P14 closes: meta-classifier dispatch + mirror-conversation
roles: engineer, technical writer, architect
mode: build session (substrate + second implementer + P14 close), executing briefs/p14/s52.md

S52 closes P14 with the meta-classifier dispatch substrate (D140), the ConversationFlow cell-payload persistence pattern (D141), and the mirror-conversation ConversationFlow implementer. The dispatch substrate resolves the three-cell-topology question deferred from S51 Finding 5: a MetaClassifier port consumes the existing StructuredOutputPort at REAL_TIME_REQUIRED tier, classifies inbounds into the four CellIdentifier values (manual_entry, audit_conversation, mirror_conversation, dispatch_clarification), and routes via the existing CellDispatch port. PendingClarification gains a `target_cell` field so confirming and resolution-ambiguity replies route to the correct cell. The mirror-conversation cell implements the third ConversationFlow at P14 close, composing the portfolio read-side substrate via a new MirrorPortfolioReader consumer port with drill-down navigation as stateless re-classification per turn against conversation history; the Message `cell_payload` JSONB column (D141) persists the cell's `current_focus_artefact` for next-turn extraction. Twelve commits land the substrate end-to-end; **P14 closes the bet's read-loop substrate.**

- Reflection (meta-classifier dispatch routing second-instance closure, prompt 1): D140's five-step dispatch flow proved correct operationally. Step 1's active-pending check via the existing PendingClarificationReader port composed cleanly with D134's substrate; Step 2's target_cell routing landed without surprise (the existing CellDispatch port from D133 absorbed the multi-cell case without contract change); Steps 3-4's MetaClassifier consumption of the StructuredOutputPort at REAL_TIME_REQUIRED tier mirrors the cell-internal intent extraction pattern from S46/S51 closely enough that the substrate's surface-area is small. Step 5's dispatch_clarification routing emerged with one structural question the brief did not fully anticipate: when the user types "mirror" (or any recognised reply) against an active dispatch_clarification pending, the use case must re-dispatch the *original* inbound text (preserved on `proposed_intent.original_inbound_text`) rather than the user's disambiguating reply. The dispatch_inbound module's `_resolve_dispatch_clarification` helper handles this by constructing a `DispatchContext` carrying the original text + intake id; the original intake id preserves audit chaining. No flow revision required; the architectural commitment held.

- Reflection (cell-payload persistence pattern at D141, prompt 2): Mirror-conversation's first use of the pattern held operationally. The implementer-side validation discipline (each implementer validates shape on read) worked cleanly via the `extract_focus_from_cell_payload` helper at `contexts/mirror_conversation/application/response.py`: malformed dicts, missing keys, unparseable UUIDs, and unrelated payload shapes (e.g., from audit-conversation outbound with cell_payload NULL) all return None, which the cell treats as no-prior-focus per the no-prior-focus edge-case commitment. No typed shape registry at messaging context surfaced as a need; the deferred-decisions entry at `charter/deferred-decisions.md` "Cell-payload schema registry at messaging context" carries the activation trigger forward for a third or fourth implementer. The cell_payload column's nullability handled audit-conversation and manual_entry cleanly without any per-cell pseudo-payload sentinel.

  2. **BroadcastDispatch substrate symmetry with CellDispatch (prompt 2).** S47's CellDispatch port handles inbound-triggered cell execution with classifier-driven routing (the dispatch_inbound use case at D140 routes via MetaClassifier). S53's BroadcastDispatch port handles trigger-driven implementer execution with deterministic routing on ``trigger_type``. The symmetric architecture held operationally cleanly: both ports have async ``dispatch`` methods; both adapters are composite (CellDispatch handles task lifecycle; BroadcastDispatch handles both registry + dispatch); both emit structured failure logs with caller-supplied context. The structural difference is routing semantics — deterministic vs classifier-driven — which is the conceptual difference between known-source triggers (scheduler, ThresholdEvaluator) versus unknown-shape inbound messages. No divergence surfaced; the architecture committed at D143 matched the implementation cleanly.

  3. **ChannelResolver static-config adapter correctness (prompt 3).** D144 commits the static-config adapter at Phase 2-A. The reactive outbound refactor at commit 6 is the first consumer.
